  persist(){
    clearTimeout(this._sv);
    this._sv = setTimeout(()=>{ this.dbPush(); const s=this.state; const payload = {
      clients:s.clients, loans:s.loans, logbook:s.logbook, petty:s.petty, contractVault:s.contractVault, txnSeq:s.txnSeq,
      employees:s.employees, prof:s.prof, twilio:s.twilio, cloud:s.cloud, attended:s.attended, theme:s.theme, ctpl:s.ctpl,
      applications:s.applications, loanSeq:s.loanSeq, clientSeq:s.clientSeq };
      try{ const str=JSON.stringify(payload); localStorage.setItem('evoltConsoleV4', str); this._savedBytes=str.length; }
      catch(e){ // quota hit — retry without heavy signature blobs so records still persist
        try{ const lite={...payload, clients:payload.clients.map(c=>c.contract&&c.contract.sig?{...c,contract:{...c.contract,sig:'[stored-separately]'}}:c)};
          localStorage.setItem('evoltConsoleV4', JSON.stringify(lite));
          this.toast('Records saved (signatures too large for local storage — use cloud vault)'); }
        catch(e2){ this.toast('⚠ Storage full — export to cloud vault to keep records'); } }
      this.sbQueue();
    }, 400);
  }
  
  sbCfg(){
    if(!this._sb){
      let c=null; try{ c=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ledjaa.supabase')||'null'); }catch(e){}
      this._sb = c || {url:'', key:'', table:'ledger_book', bookId:'main', connected:false, lastSync:null, lastErr:null};
    }
    return this._sb;
  }
  sbSave(patch){
    this._sb = {...this.sbCfg(), ...patch};
    try{ localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.supabase', JSON.stringify(this._sb)); }catch(e){}
    this.forceUpdate();
  }
  sbBase(){ const c=this.sbCfg(); return String(c.url||'').replace(/\/+$/,''); }
  sbHeaders(extra){
    const c=this.sbCfg();
    return Object.assign({'apikey':c.key,'Authorization':'Bearer '+c.key,'Content-Type':'application/json'}, extra||{});
  }
  sbPayload(){
    const s=this.state;
    return { clients:s.clients, loans:s.loans, logbook:s.logbook, petty:s.petty, contractVault:s.contractVault,
      txnSeq:s.txnSeq, bank:this.bankCfg(), bankTx:this.bankTx(), expenses:this.expList(), statusAt:s.statusAt||{}, attended:s.attended||{}, clientRefHigh:(()=>{ try{ return parseInt(localStorage.getItem('ledjaa.clientRefHigh')||'0',10)||0; }catch(e){ return 0; } })(), schema:'ledger-book/2', employees:s.employees, prof:s.prof, applications:s.applications, loanSeq:s.loanSeq, clientSeq:s.clientSeq };
  }
  async sbPush(silent){
    const c=this.sbCfg();
    if(!c.connected || !c.url || !c.key) return;
    this.sbSave({syncing:true});
    try{
      const body=[{id:c.bookId, payload:this.sbPayload(), updated_at:new Date().toISOString(), updated_by:(this.state.prof&&this.state.prof.name)||'unknown'}];
      const r=await fetch(this.sbBase()+'/rest/v1/'+c.table+'?on_conflict=id', {
        method:'POST', headers:this.sbHeaders({'Prefer':'resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal'}), body:JSON.stringify(body)});
      if(!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP '+r.status+' '+(await r.text()).slice(0,140));
      this.sbSave({syncing:false, lastSync:new Date().toISOString(), lastErr:null});
      if(!silent) this.toast('Book saved to cloud');
    }catch(e){
      this.sbSave({syncing:false, lastErr:String(e.message||e)});
      if(!silent) this.toast('Cloud save failed — '+String(e.message||e).slice(0,70));
    }
  }
  async sbPull(){
    const c=this.sbCfg();
    if(!c.url || !c.key) return this.toast('Enter your project URL and key first');
    this.sbSave({syncing:true});
    try{
      const r=await fetch(this.sbBase()+'/rest/v1/'+c.table+'?id=eq.'+encodeURIComponent(c.bookId)+'&select=payload,updated_at,updated_by', {headers:this.sbHeaders()});
      if(!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP '+r.status+' '+(await r.text()).slice(0,140));
      const rows=await r.json();
      if(!rows.length) throw new Error('No book stored yet — push first');
      const d=rows[0].payload||{};
      if(!d.clients||!d.loans) throw new Error('Stored book is missing records');
      try{
        if(d.bank){ this._bank=d.bank; localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.bank', JSON.stringify(d.bank)); }
        if(Array.isArray(d.bankTx)){ this._btx=d.bankTx; localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.bank.tx', JSON.stringify(d.bankTx)); }
        if(Array.isArray(d.expenses)){ this._exp=d.expenses; localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.expenses', JSON.stringify(d.expenses)); }
        if(d.clientRefHigh) localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.clientRefHigh', String(Math.max(d.clientRefHigh, parseInt(localStorage.getItem('ledjaa.clientRefHigh')||'0',10)||0)));
        const snap={}; d.loans.forEach(l=>snap[l.id]=l.status); this._bankSnap=snap; localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.bank.snap', JSON.stringify(snap));
      }catch(e){}
      this.setState(p=>({statusAt:d.statusAt||p.statusAt, attended:d.attended||p.attended, clients:d.clients, loans:d.loans, logbook:d.logbook||p.logbook, petty:d.petty||p.petty,
        contractVault:d.contractVault||p.contractVault, employees:d.employees||p.employees, applications:d.applications||p.applications,
        txnSeq:d.txnSeq||p.txnSeq, loanSeq:d.loanSeq||p.loanSeq, clientSeq:d.clientSeq||p.clientSeq}));
      this.sbSave({syncing:false, lastSync:rows[0].updated_at, lastErr:null, connected:true});
      this.toast('Pulled '+d.clients.length+' clients, '+d.loans.length+' loans from cloud');
    }catch(e){
      this.sbSave({syncing:false, lastErr:String(e.message||e)});
      this.toast('Cloud pull failed — '+String(e.message||e).slice(0,70));
    }
  }
  async sbConnect(){
    const c=this.sbCfg();
    if(c.connected){ this.sbSave({connected:false}); return this.toast('Cloud sync paused'); }
    if(!/^https:\/\/.+/.test(c.url||'') || !c.key) return this.toast('Enter your project URL and anon key');
    this.sbSave({syncing:true});
    try{
      const r=await fetch(this.sbBase()+'/rest/v1/'+c.table+'?select=id&limit=1', {headers:this.sbHeaders()});
      if(!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP '+r.status+' '+(await r.text()).slice(0,140));
      const ex = await fetch(this.sbBase()+'/rest/v1/'+c.table+'?id=eq.'+encodeURIComponent(c.bookId)+'&select=updated_at', {headers:this.sbHeaders()});
      const exRows = ex.ok ? await ex.json() : [];
      this.sbSave({syncing:false, connected:true, lastErr:null});
      try{ const L=this.q1Apps(), sa=L.find(a=>/supabase/i.test(a.name)); if(sa && !sa.url) this.q1Patch(sa.id,{url:c.url.replace(/\/+$/,''), key:c.key, path:'/rest/v1/'+(c.table||'ledger_book')+'?select=id,updated_at,updated_by', on:true}); }catch(e){}
      if(exRows.length){ this.toast('Connected — loading the book stored in Supabase'); await this.sbPull(); }
      else { this.toast('Connected — uploading this book to Supabase'); this.sbPush(true); }
    }catch(e){
      this.sbSave({syncing:false, connected:false, lastErr:String(e.message||e)});
      this.toast('Could not reach the table — '+String(e.message||e).slice(0,70));
    }
  }
  sbQueue(){
    if(!this.sbCfg().connected) return;
    clearTimeout(this._sbT);
    this._sbT = setTimeout(()=>this.sbPush(true), 2500);
  }
  resetDemo(){ try{ localStorage.setItem('evoltConsoleV4','null'); }catch(e){} location.reload(); }
  initDb(){
    import((window.__resources&&window.__resources.ldbStandalone)||new URL('./ledjaa-db.js', location.href).href).then(m=>{
      this.ldb = m.db; this.ldbMod = m;
      const actor = m.db.table('users').find(u=>u.role==='admin');
      if(actor) m.db.setActor(actor.id);
      const s = m.db.toConsoleState();
      this.csvLock(m, s);
      if(s.clients.length){
        const keep = this.state.clients.reduce((o,c)=>{ o[c.id]=c.contract; return o; }, {});
        this.setState({
          clients: s.clients.map((c,i)=>({...c, contract: keep[c.id] || {no:'MC-00'+(31+i), signedOn:c.added, sig:null}})),
          loans: s.loans.map(l=>({...l, ret:(l.ret==null?35:l.ret)})), employees: s.employees, dbReady:true,
          prof: actor ? {name:actor.name, email:actor.email, role:'Administrator'} : this.state.prof
        });
        setTimeout(()=>this.maybeBrief(), 350);
      } else this.setState({dbReady:true, prof: actor ? {name:actor.name, email:actor.email, role:'Administrator'} : this.state.prof});
    }).catch(e=>{ console.warn('Shared data layer unavailable:', e); this.setState({dbReady:false, dbError:true}); });
  }
  // Uploaded CSV figures are authoritative. Restore principal + instalment from the seed (the CSV) wherever they drifted.
  async csvLock(m, s){
    let seed=null; try{ seed=(await import((window.__resources&&window.__resources.ldbStandalone)||new URL('./ledjaa-seed.js', location.href).href)).SEED_LOANS; }catch(e){ return; }
    const byRef={}; (seed||[]).forEach(x=>{ byRef[x.ref]=x; });
    const fixed=[];
    const fix = arr => (arr||[]).map(l=>{ const c=byRef[l.ref]; if(!c) return l; if(c.mandate && l.mandate && String(c.mandate)!==String(l.mandate)) return l;
      const sched=(c.sched||[]).map(x=>{ const p=x.split('-'); return p.length===3 ? (+p[2])+' '+['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'][+p[1]-1]+' '+p[0] : x; });
      const want={amount:c.amount, retAmt:c.retAmt, instDone:c.instDone||0, sched};
      if(l.amount!==want.amount || l.retAmt!==want.retAmt || (l.instDone||0)!==want.instDone || JSON.stringify(l.sched)!==JSON.stringify(sched)){ fixed.push(l.ref); return this.instNorm({...l, ...want}); } return l; });
    s.loans = fix(s.loans);
    setTimeout(()=>{ const have=this.state.loans||[], toLbl=x=>{ const p=x.split('-'); return p.length===3 ? (+p[2])+' '+['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'][+p[1]-1]+' '+p[0] : x; };
      const miss=(seed||[]).filter(x=>x.mandate && !have.some(l=>String(l.mandate||'')===String(x.mandate)) && !have.some(l=>l.ref===x.ref));
      if(!miss.length) return;
      const add=miss.map(x=>this.instNorm({...x, id:x.id+'_'+x.mandate, sched:(x.sched||[]).map(toLbl), paidInst:x.status==='paid'?(x.sched||[]).length:0}));
      this.setState(p=>({loans:[...p.loans, ...add], statusAt:{...(p.statusAt||{}), ...add.reduce((o,l)=>(o[l.id]=Date.now(),o),{})}}));
      add.forEach(l=>{ const c=(this.state.clients||[]).find(k=>k.id===l.clientId)||{}; this.logAdd({ref:l.ref, customer:c.name||'—', amount:l.amount, type:'LOAN ADDED', date:this.todayStamp(), by:(this.state.prof&&this.state.prof.name)||'—', comment:'Mandate '+l.mandate+' · from NuPay transaction report 22–25 Oct 2026'}); });
      console.info('CSV loans added:', add.map(l=>l.ref).join(', ')); }, 600);
    (m.db.rows('loans')||[]).forEach(r=>{ const c=byRef[r.ref]; if(c && (r.principal!==c.amount || r.instalment!==c.retAmt)) m.db.update('loans', r.id, {principal:c.amount, instalment:c.retAmt}); });
    const cur = fix(this.state.loans);
    if(fixed.length){ this.setState({loans:cur.map(l=>this.instNorm(l))}); console.warn('CSV lock restored:', [...new Set(fixed)].join(', ')); }
  }
  instNorm(l){
    if(!l) return l;
    const sched=(l.sched&&l.sched.length)?l.sched:(l.due?[l.due]:[]), n=sched.length, done=l.instDone||0;
    let paid=Math.min(l.paidInst||0, n), status=l.status;
    if(status==='paid' && paid<n){ paid+=1; if(paid<n) status='issued'; }
    if(paid>=n && n>0 && status!=='written off') status='paid';
    const idx=Math.min(paid, Math.max(n-1,0));
    return {...l, status, paidInst:paid, due: sched[idx]||l.due, instNo: done+idx+1, instTotal: done+n,
      instRemaining: Math.max(n-paid,0), nextDue: (paid<n && sched[idx+1]) ? sched[idx+1] : null};
  }
  instSync(){
    const ls=this.state.loans||[]; let ch=false;
    const out=ls.map(l=>{ const k=this.instNorm(l);
      if(k.status!==l.status||k.paidInst!==l.paidInst||k.due!==l.due||k.instNo!==l.instNo||k.instTotal!==l.instTotal||k.nextDue!==l.nextDue){ ch=true; return k; } return l; });
    if(ch) this.setState({loans:out});
  }
  dbIso(d){ const t=this.slParse(d); if(!t) return null;
    return t.getFullYear()+'-'+String(t.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(t.getDate()).padStart(2,'0'); }
  dbPush(){
    const db=this.ldb; if(!db || !this.state.dbReady) return;
    try{
      this.state.clients.forEach(c=>{
        const row=db.find('clients', c.id);
        const patch={ ref:c.ref, name:c.name, id_number:c.idFull==='—'?null:c.idFull, email:c.email==='—'?null:c.email,
          phone:c.phone==='—'?null:c.phone, address:c.address==='—'?null:c.address, city:c.city==='—'?null:c.city,
          province:c.province==='—'?null:c.province, employer:c.employer==='—'?null:c.employer,
          job_title:c.jobTitle==='—'?null:c.jobTitle, net_salary:c.netSalary||0, bank:c.bank==='—'?null:c.bank,
          branch_code:c.branchCode==='—'?null:c.branchCode, account_no:c.account==='—'?null:c.account,
          account_type:c.accountType==='—'?null:c.accountType, score:c.score||0, blocked:!!c.blocked, blocked_at:c.blockedAt||null };
        if(row) db.update('clients', c.id, patch);
        else db.insert('clients', {id:c.id, ...patch, dob:null, employment:null, tenure:null, officer_id:null,
          branch_id:null, created_at:new Date().toISOString(), retain_until:null, deleted_at:null});
      });
      const roleEnum = (label)=>{
        const k = String(label||'').trim().toLowerCase();
        if(k.startsWith('admin')) return 'admin';
        if(k.startsWith('manager')) return 'manager';
        if(k.startsWith('collect')) return 'collections';
        if(k.startsWith('audit')) return 'auditor';
        if(k==='client') return 'client';
        return 'agent';
      };
      (this.state.employees||[]).forEach(e=>{
        const row=db.find('users', e.id);
        const patch={ name:e.name, email:e.email, role:roleEnum(e.role), active:String(e.status||'ACTIVE').toUpperCase()!=='DISABLED' };
        if(row) db.update('users', e.id, patch);
        else db.insert('users', {id:e.id, ...patch, branch_id:null, client_id:null, approval_limit:0,
          created_at:new Date().toISOString(), last_login_at:null, relogin_required_at:null});
      });
      this.state.loans.forEach(l=>{
        const row=db.find('loans', l.id);
        const patch={ ref:l.ref, client_id:l.clientId, principal:l.amount, return_rate_pct:l.ret, status:l.status,
          due_date:this.dbIso(l.due), purpose:l.purpose,
          instalment: l.retAmt!=null ? l.retAmt : Math.round(l.amount*(1+(l.ret||35)/100)*100)/100,
          schedule: (l.sched&&l.sched.length) ? l.sched.map(d=>this.dbIso(d)||d) : ((row&&row.schedule)||undefined),
          issued_on: l.issued ? this.dbIso(l.issued) : ((row&&row.issued_on)||null), issued_by: l.issuedBy||(row&&row.issued_by)||null,
          employee_id: l.employeeId||(row&&row.employee_id)||null, mandate_id: l.mandate||(row&&row.mandate_id)||null };
        patch.instalments_paid = l.paidInst||0;
        if(row){ delete patch.principal; delete patch.instalment; delete patch.return_rate_pct;
          if(patch.schedule && JSON.stringify(patch.schedule)===JSON.stringify(row.schedule)) delete patch.schedule;
          db.update('loans', l.id, patch); }
        else db.insert('loans', {id:l.id, ...patch, application_id:null, term_months:(patch.schedule&&patch.schedule.length)||1, instalments_paid:0, initiation_fee:0, service_fee:0,
          disbursed_on:patch.issued_on||null, parent_loan_id:null, relation:'original', written_off_at:null,
          write_off_reason:null, written_off_by:null, officer_id:null, branch_id:null,
          created_at:new Date().toISOString(), deleted_at:null});
      });
      // records removed in the console are archived (soft-deleted), never left to reappear on reload
      const liveL={}, liveC={}; this.state.loans.forEach(l=>liveL[l.id]=1); this.state.clients.forEach(c=>liveC[c.id]=1);
      db.rows('loans').forEach(r=>{ if(!liveL[r.id]) try{ db.softDelete('loans', r.id, 'Removed in console'); }catch(e){} });
      db.rows('clients').forEach(r=>{ if(!liveC[r.id]) try{ db.softDelete('clients', r.id, 'Removed in console'); }catch(e){} });
    }catch(e){ console.warn('DB sync skipped:', e.message); }
  }
  roleScope(st){
    const role = st.viewRole || 'admin';
    if(role==='admin' || role==='manager' || role==='auditor') return st;
    if(role==='agent'){
      const acting = this.ldb ? (this.ldb.table('users').find(u=>u.role==='agent')||{}).name : null;
      const me = acting || st.prof.name;
      const clients = st.clients.filter(c=>c.employee===me);
      const ids = clients.reduce((o,c)=>{ o[c.id]=1; return o; },{});
      return {...st, clients, loans: st.loans.filter(l=>ids[l.clientId])};
    }
    if(role==='collections') return {...st, loans: st.loans.filter(l=>l.status!=='paid')};
    return st;
  }
  roleVals(){
    const cur = this.state.viewRole || 'admin';
    const defs = [['admin','ADMIN'],['manager','MANAGER'],['agent','AGENT'],['collections','COLLECTIONS'],['auditor','AUDITOR']];
    const notes = { admin:'Full book · every branch', manager:'Full book · approvals and write-offs',
      agent:(this.ldb ? ((this.ldb.table('users').find(u=>u.role==='agent')||{}).name || 'Agent') : 'Agent')+"'s own clients · no approvals",
      collections:'Unpaid loans only · payments and mandates',
      auditor:'Read-only · full book and audit trail' };
    const db = this.ldb;
    const rows = db ? Object.values(db.counts()).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0) : 0;
    return {
      roleTabs: defs.map(([id,label])=>({ label, go:()=>this.setState({viewRole:id, menuId:null}),
        style: 'padding:5px 10px;border-radius:5px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;'
          + "font:600 10px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif;letter-spacing:.09em;"
          + (cur===id ? 'background:var(--acc);color:var(--accInk)' : 'color:var(--mut);border:1px solid var(--line2)') })),
      roleScopeNote: notes[cur] || '',
      dbStatus: this.state.dbError ? 'DATABASE UNAVAILABLE'
        : (db ? 'ECOLOANS DATABASE · ' + rows.toLocaleString('en-ZA') + ' ROWS' : 'CONNECTING TO DATABASE'),
      dbDotColor: this.state.dbError ? 'var(--red)' : (db ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--amber)')
    };
  }
  reelCfg(){ try{ return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ledjaa.reel')||'null')||{}; }catch(e){ return {}; } }
  async loadReel(force){
    const day = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10), c = this.reelCfg();
    if(c.fb){ this.setState({reel:{kind:'fb', url:c.fb, title:c.fbTitle||'The Economist · Facebook reel', at:c.fbAt}}); return; }
    if(!force && c.day===day && c.yt && c.list){ this.setState({reel:c.list[this.state.reelIdx||0]||c.yt, reelList:c.list}); return; }
    try{
      const r = await fetch('https://api.rss2json.com/v1/api.json?rss_url='+encodeURIComponent('https://www.youtube.com/feeds/videos.xml?channel_id=UC0p5jTq6Xx_DosDFxVXnWaQ'));
      const j = await r.json();
      const list = (j.items||[]).map(it=>{ const m = String(it.link||'').match(/(?:v=|shorts\/|youtu\.be\/)([\w-]{11})/) || String(it.guid||'').match(/([\w-]{11})$/); return m ? {kind:'yt', id:m[1], title:it.title, at:it.pubDate} : null; }).filter(Boolean);
      if(!list.length) throw 0;
      const yt = list[0];
      localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.reel', JSON.stringify({...c, day, yt, list}));
      this.setState({reel:yt, reelList:list, reelIdx:0});
    }catch(e){ if(c.yt) this.setState({reel:c.yt, reelList:c.list||[c.yt]}); }
  }
  reelTo(n){ const L=this.state.reelList||[]; if(!L[n]) return;
    this.setState({reel:L[n], reelIdx:n, reelPlay:false}, ()=>{ const el=this._reelStrip; if(!el) return; const th=el.querySelectorAll('[title]')[n]; if(!th) return;
      el.scrollTo({left: th.offsetLeft - (el.clientWidth - th.offsetWidth)/2, behavior:'smooth'}); }); }
  componentDidMount(){ setTimeout(()=>this.loadReel(),300); setTimeout(()=>this.qkSync(),2500); this._qkT=setInterval(()=>{ if(!document.hidden) this.qkSync(); },60000);
    this._reelAuto = setInterval(()=>{ const st=this.state, L=st.reelList||[];
      if(st.reelPlay || this._reelHover || L.length<2 || document.hidden || st.route!=='dashboard') return;
      if(this._reelPause && Date.now()-this._reelPause < 12000) return;
      const i=Math.max(0,L.findIndex(x=>st.reel&&x.id===st.reel.id)); this.reelTo((i+1)%L.length); }, 5000); this._reelT=setInterval(()=>this.loadReel(),60*60e3); setTimeout(()=>this.loadNews(),400); this._newsT=setInterval(()=>this.loadNews(true),15*60e3); this.initDb(); this.applyTheme(); try{ localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.theme.v3', this.state.theme); }catch(e){} document.addEventListener('click', this.onDocClick = () => { if(this.state.menuId) this.setState({menuId:null}); }); }
  
  maybeBrief(){
    let seen = null;
    try{ seen = sessionStorage.getItem('ledjaaBriefSeen'); }catch(e){}
    const today = this.todayLabel();
    if(seen === today) return;
    try{ sessionStorage.setItem('ledjaaBriefSeen', today); }catch(e){}
    this.botDailyBrief();
  }
  botDailyBrief(){
    const st=this.state, f=n=>this.fmt(n), cn=id=>((st.clients||[]).find(c=>c.id===id)||{}).name||'—';
    const first=((st.prof&&st.prof.name)||'').split(' ')[0]||'there';
    const hr=new Date().getHours(), greet=hr<12?'Good morning':hr<17?'Good afternoon':'Good evening';
    const notif=this.notifItems(), due=notif.filter(n=>n.kind==='today'), flag=notif.filter(n=>n.flagged);
    const dueT=due.reduce((a,n)=>a+this.dueAmt(n.loan),0);
    const overdue=(st.loans||[]).filter(l=>l.status==='overdue');
    const bn=this.bankNow();
    const apps=(st.applications||[]).filter(a=>a.status==='new').length;
    const MN=['JAN','FEB','MAR','APR','MAY','JUN','JUL','AUG','SEP','OCT','NOV','DEC'], mk=this.expMonthKey();
    const t=this.today(), exDue=this.expList().filter(e=>!(e.paid||{})[mk] && (e.day||1)===t.getDate());
    const nxt=this.nextCollectionDay();
    const L=[greet+', '+first+'. Here is today, '+this.todayHeader()+'.',''];
    if(due.length){ L.push('Collecting today: '+due.length+(due.length===1?' debit order':' debit orders')+' · '+f(dueT)); due.forEach(n=>L.push('• '+n.loan.ref+' '+cn(n.loan.clientId)+' · '+f(this.dueAmt(n.loan))+(n.total>1?' (inst '+n.instNo+'/'+n.total+')':''))); }
    else L.push('Nothing collects today.'+(nxt?' Next collection: '+nxt+'.':''));
    if(flag.length){ L.push(''); L.push('Needs updating: '+flag.length+' past collection'+(flag.length===1?'':'s')+' still not marked'); flag.slice(0,4).forEach(n=>L.push('• '+n.loan.ref+' '+cn(n.loan.clientId)+' · due '+n.date)); }
    if(overdue.length){ L.push(''); L.push('Overdue: '+overdue.length+(overdue.length===1?' loan':' loans')+' · '+f(overdue.reduce((a,l)=>a+this.dueAmt(l),0))); }
    L.push('');
    L.push('Bank balance: '+(bn.bal==null?'not set':f(bn.bal)));
    if(apps) L.push('New applications waiting: '+apps);
    if(exDue.length) L.push('Expenses due today: '+exDue.map(e=>e.name+' '+f(e.amount)).join(', '));
    const act = (flag.length || this.bulkItems().length) ? {go:'bulkstatus', label:'Update statuses'} : due.length ? {go:'filter:duetoday', label:'View today\'s collections'} : null;
    this.botSaveHist([...this.botHist(), {role:'bot', text:L.join('\n'), act, at:Date.now(), brief:true, alert:(due.length+flag.length+overdue.length)>0}]);
    this.setState({botOpen:true});
  }
  clampMenu(){
    const el = this._menuEl; if(!el || !this.state.menuId) return;
    const pad = 12, r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    const vw = window.innerWidth, vh = window.innerHeight;
    let dx = 0, dy = 0;
    if(r.right > vw - pad) dx = (vw - pad) - r.right;
    if(r.left + dx < pad) dx = pad - r.left;
    if(r.bottom > vh - pad) dy = (vh - pad) - r.bottom;
    if(r.top + dy < pad) dy = pad - r.top;
    if(dx || dy){
      el.style.left = Math.round(r.left + dx) + 'px';
      el.style.top = Math.round(r.top + dy) + 'px';
    }
  }

  hiResImg(u){
    if(!u) return u;
    let x = String(u).replace(/&amp;/g,'&');
    x = x.replace(/(ichef\.bbci\.co\.uk\/(?:ace\/(?:standard|ws)|news))\/\d+\//, '$1/1024/');
    x = x.replace(/-\d{2,4}x\d{2,4}(\.(?:jpe?g|png|webp))/i, '$1');
    x = x.replace(/([?&])(resize|w|h|width|height|fit|crop|quality|q)=[^&]*/gi, '$1').replace(/[?&]+$/,'').replace(/\?&+/,'?');
    if(/image\.cnbcfm\.com/.test(x)) x = x.split('?')[0] + '?w=1200';
    return x;
  }
  bestImg(it){
    const html = String(it.content||'')+String(it.description||'');
    let best = '', bw = 0;
    (html.match(/srcset=["']([^"']+)["']/gi)||[]).forEach(ss=>{
      ss.replace(/^srcset=["']|["']$/g,'').split(',').forEach(c=>{ const p=c.trim().split(/\s+/); const w=parseInt(p[1])||0; if(p[0]&&w>bw){ bw=w; best=p[0]; } });
    });
    const enc = it.enclosure && (it.enclosure.link||'');
    const encOk = enc && /\.(jpe?g|png|webp)|image/i.test(enc+(it.enclosure.type||''));
    const imgTag = (html.match(/<img[^>]+src=["']([^"']+)/i)||[])[1];
    return this.hiResImg(best || (encOk?enc:'') || imgTag || it.thumbnail || '');
  }
  newsCat(title, base){
    const t=String(title||'');
    if(/petrol|diesel|fuel|brent|crude|opec|oil price|pump price|paraffin|lpg/i.test(t)) return 'fuel';
    if(/jse|share|stock|nasdaq|s&p|dow|equit|ftse|nikkei|earnings|ipo|investor/i.test(t)) return 'stocks';
    return base||'markets';
  }
  async loadNews(force){
    const cached = (()=>{ try{ return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ledjaa.news')||'null'); }catch(e){ return null; } })();
    if(cached && cached.items && cached.items.length && cached.items[0].desc===undefined) force = true;
    if(cached && cached.items && !cached.items.some(i=>i.cat==='global')) force = true;
    if(!force && cached && cached.items && Date.now()-cached.at < 15*60e3){ this.setState({news:{...cached, loading:false}}); if(!cached.brief) this.newsBrief(cached.items); return; }
    this.setState(p=>({news:{...(p.news||cached||{items:[]}), loading:true, err:null}}));
    const strip = h => String(h||'').replace(/<[^>]*>/g,'').replace(/&[a-z#0-9]+;/gi,' ').trim();
    const imgFrom = it => it.thumbnail || (it.enclosure && (it.enclosure.link||it.enclosure.thumbnail)) || ((String(it.content||it.description||'').match(/<img[^>]+src=["']([^"']+)/i)||[])[1]) || '';
    const results = await Promise.all(this.newsFeeds().map(async f=>{
      try{
        const r = await fetch('https://api.rss2json.com/v1/api.json?rss_url='+encodeURIComponent(f.url));
        if(!r.ok) return [];
        const j = await r.json();
        if(j.status!=='ok') return [];
        return (j.items||[]).filter(it=>!f.only || f.only.test(it.title+' '+(it.description||''))).slice(0,8).map(it=>({
          title: strip(it.title), link: it.link, src: f.src, img: this.bestImg(it) || imgFrom(it), desc: strip(it.description||it.content).replace(/\s+/g,' ').replace(/(The post .*appeared first on.*$)|(Continue reading.*$)/i,'').trim().slice(0,420),
          at: Date.parse(String(it.pubDate||'').replace(' ','T')+'Z') || Date.parse(it.pubDate) || Date.now(),
          cat: f.cat==='global' ? 'global' : this.newsCat(it.title, f.cat), sa: !!f.sa
        }));
      }catch(e){ return []; }
    }));
    const seen = {}, all = results.flat().filter(n=>n.title && n.link && !seen[n.title] && (seen[n.title]=1)).sort((a,b)=>((b.sa?1:0)-(a.sa?1:0)) || (b.at-a.at));
    const items = [...all.filter(n=>n.cat!=='global').slice(0,32), ...all.filter(n=>n.cat==='global').slice(0,18)];
    if(!items.length){ this.setState(p=>({news:{...(p.news||{}), loading:false, err:'Could not reach the news feeds right now.'}})); return; }
    const news = {items, at:Date.now(), brief:null};
    try{ localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.news', JSON.stringify(news)); }catch(e){}
    this.setState({news:{...news, loading:false, err:null}});
    this.newsBrief(items);
  }
  async newsBrief(items){
    if(!(window.claude && window.claude.complete) || !items || !items.length) return;
    try{
      const heads = items.slice(0,12).map(n=>'- ['+n.cat+'] '+n.title+' ('+n.src+')').join('\n');
      const out = await window.claude.complete('You brief a South African micro-lender each morning. From these headlines only, write ONE plain sentence (max 35 words) on what matters today in South Africa: the rand, JSE, SARB rates, household credit and local fuel prices. No preamble, no speculation beyond the headlines.\n\n'+heads);
      const brief = String(out||'').trim().slice(0,260);
      if(!brief) return;
      this.setState(p=>({news:{...(p.news||{}), brief}}));
      try{ const c=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ledjaa.news')||'null'); if(c){ c.brief=brief; localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.news', JSON.stringify(c)); } }catch(e){}
    }catch(e){}
  }

  cardOrder(){
    if(!this._cardOrder){
      let saved = null;
      try{ saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ledjaa.cardOrder.v3')||'null'); }catch(e){}
      this._cardOrder = (Array.isArray(saved) && saved.length===3 && [0,1,2].every(i=>saved.indexOf(i)>=0)) ? saved : [2,0,1];
    }
    return this._cardOrder;
  }
  applyCardOrder(){
    const g = this._gridEl; if(!g) return;
    const ord = this.cardOrder();
    ord.forEach((cardId,slot)=>{
      const el = g.querySelector('[data-card="'+cardId+'"]');
      if(el){ el.style.order = String(slot); el.style.cursor = this.state.arrangeMode?'grab':''; }
    });
  }
  componentDidUpdate(){ this.instSync(); this.persist(); this.clampMenu(); this.applyCardOrder(); this.bankTrack(); }
  fmt(n){ const v=Number(n||0), neg=v<0, a=Math.abs(v);
    const whole=Math.floor(a+1e-9), cents=Math.round((a-whole)*100);
    const grouped=String(whole).replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g,' ');
    return (neg?'-R ':'R ') + grouped + (cents?'.'+String(cents).padStart(2,'0'):''); }
  toast(msg){ clearTimeout(this._tt); this.setState({toast:msg}); this._tt = setTimeout(()=>this.setState({toast:null}), 2600); }
  menuPos(r){
    const W=214, H=292, pad=12;
    let left = r ? r.right - W : 0, top = r ? r.bottom + 6 : 0;
    if(r){
      if(top + H > window.innerHeight - pad) top = Math.max(pad, r.top - H - 6);
      left = Math.max(pad, Math.min(left, window.innerWidth - W - pad));
    }
    return this.cssObj('position:fixed;left:'+Math.round(left)+'px;top:'+Math.round(top)+'px;z-index:120;box-sizing:border-box;width:'+W+'px;background:var(--panel2);border:1px solid var(--line2);border-radius:12px;box-shadow:var(--shadow);padding:6px;animation:fadeUp .12s ease');
  }
  today(){ const d=new Date(); d.setHours(0,0,0,0); return d; }
  todayLabel(){ const d=this.today(), MN=['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'];
    return d.getDate()+' '+MN[d.getMonth()]+' '+d.getFullYear(); }
  todayStamp(){ return this.todayLabel().toUpperCase(); }
  todayHeader(){ const d=this.today(), DW=['SUN','MON','TUE','WED','THU','FRI','SAT'];
    return DW[d.getDay()]+' '+this.todayStamp(); }
  briefFor(dayKey){
    const st = this.state, MN=['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'];
    const rows = [];
    st.loans.forEach(l=>{
      if(l.status==='paid' || l.status==='written off') return;
      const dates = (l.sched && l.sched.length ? l.sched : [l.due]);
      const k=this.slParse(dayKey), kt=k?new Date(k.getFullYear(),k.getMonth(),k.getDate()).getTime():null;
      dates.forEach((dstr,i)=>{ const p=this.slParse(dstr); const pt=p?new Date(p.getFullYear(),p.getMonth(),p.getDate()).getTime():null; if(dstr===dayKey || (kt!=null&&pt===kt)) rows.push({loan:l, instNo:i+1, total:dates.length}); });
    });
    return rows;
  }

    return this._bank;
  }

  nextClientRef(clients){
    const nums = (clients||this.state.clients||[]).map(c=>{ const m=String(c.ref||'').match(/(\d+)\s*$/); return m?parseInt(m[1],10):0; });
    let hi=0; try{ hi=parseInt(localStorage.getItem('ledjaa.clientRefHigh')||'0',10)||0; }catch(e){}
    const n = Math.max(hi, ...nums, 0) + 1;
    try{ localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.clientRefHigh', String(n)); }catch(e){}
    return 'EC-0'+n;
  }
