  botContext(){
    const st=this.state, f=n=>this.fmt(n), cn=id=>((st.clients||[]).find(c=>c.id===id)||{});
    const notif=this.notifItems(), bn=this.bankNow(), ex=this.expList();
    const loans=(st.loans||[]).map(l=>{ const c=cn(l.clientId); return l.ref+' | '+(c.name||'—')+' ('+(c.ref||'')+(c.blocked?', BLOCKED':'')+') | lent '+(this.lentOf(l)==null?'not recorded':f(this.lentOf(l)))+' | return per instalment '+f(this.dueAmt(l))+(this.isCsvLoan(l)?' | client debit '+f(l.retAmt)+' incl NuPay PIF '+f(this.pifAmt(l)):'')+' x'+(l.instTotal||(l.sched&&l.sched.length)||1)+' | status '+l.status+' | dates '+((l.sched&&l.sched.length?l.sched:[l.due]).join(', ')); }).join('\n');
    return 'TODAY: '+this.todayHeader()+'\nUSER: '+((st.prof&&st.prof.name)||'—')+'\nCURRENT PAGE: '+st.route+
      '\nBANK BALANCE: '+(bn.bal==null?'not set':f(bn.bal))+
      '\nDUE TODAY: '+notif.filter(n=>n.kind==='today').map(n=>n.loan.ref+' '+f(this.dueAmt(n.loan))).join('; ')+
      '\nFLAGGED (past due, status not updated): '+notif.filter(n=>n.flagged).map(n=>n.loan.ref+' due '+n.date).join('; ')+
      '\nMONTHLY OFFICE EXPENSES: '+ex.map(e=>e.name+' '+f(e.amount)).join('; ')+
      '\nEMPLOYEES: '+(st.employees||[]).map(e=>e.name+' ('+(e.role||'')+', '+(e.status||'')+')').join('; ')+
      '\nCLIENTS: '+(st.clients||[]).length+'\nLOANS (ref | client | principal | CSV instalment x count | status | instalment dates):\n'+loans;
  }
  q1Apps(){ if(!this._q1a){ let a=null; try{ a=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('q1.apps')||'null'); }catch(e){}
      const P=[['Quik1 loan app','/applications?status=new','Q1 checks this every minute and brings new loan applications into Applications. Allow actions to send approved / declined back to Quik1.'],['NuPay (debit orders)','/transactions?days=7','NuPay debit-order results. Most NuPay endpoints need a server proxy.'],['Bank account','/accounts/balance','Bank or Stitch / Ozow API for balance and statement lines. Usually needs a proxy.'],['Supabase / own database','/rest/v1/ledger_book?select=*','Supabase REST works from the browser. Use the anon key.'],['Accounting (Xero, Sage)','/Invoices?where=Status=="AUTHORISED"','Xero or Sage. OAuth tokens expire; use a proxy that refreshes them.'],['WhatsApp / SMS','/messages?limit=20','WhatsApp Cloud API or SMS gateway. Actions send messages.'],['Google Sheets','','Paste a published-to-web CSV link or an Apps Script web-app URL as the base URL.']];
      this._q1a = Array.isArray(a) ? a : P.map((p,i)=>({id:'q'+i, name:p[0], url:'', path:p[1], key:'', proxy:'', on:false, actions:false, hint:p[2], pathHint:p[1]||'(leave blank)'})); }
    return this._q1a; }
  q1Save(list){ this._q1a=list; try{ localStorage.setItem('q1.apps', JSON.stringify(list)); }catch(e){} this.forceUpdate(); }
  q1Patch(id, p){ this.q1Save(this.q1Apps().map(a=>a.id===id?{...a,...p}:a)); }
  q1Url(a, path){ const u=String(a.url||'').replace(/\/+$/,'')+(path?('/'+String(path).replace(/^\/+/,'')):''); return a.proxy ? String(a.proxy).replace(/\/+$/,'')+'?url='+encodeURIComponent(u) : u; }
  async q1Req(a, method, path, body){
    const ctl=new AbortController(), t=setTimeout(()=>ctl.abort(), 6000), hd={'Accept':'application/json, text/csv, */*'};
    if(a.key){ hd['Authorization']='Bearer '+a.key; if(/supabase/i.test(a.url)) hd['apikey']=a.key; }
    if(body!=null) hd['Content-Type']='application/json';
    try{ const r=await fetch(this.q1Url(a, path), {method:method||'GET', headers:hd, body:body!=null?JSON.stringify(body):undefined, signal:ctl.signal});
      const tx=await r.text(); if(!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP '+r.status+' '+tx.slice(0,120)); return tx; }
    catch(e){ throw new Error(e.name==='AbortError'?'Timed out after 6s':(/Failed to fetch/i.test(String(e.message))?'Blocked by the app (CORS). Add a proxy URL.':String(e.message||e))); }
    finally{ clearTimeout(t); } }
  async q1Context(){ const live=this.q1Apps().filter(a=>a.on && a.url); if(!live.length) return '';
    const res=await Promise.all(live.map(async a=>{ try{ const tx=await this.q1Req(a,'GET',a.path); return '### '+a.name+(a.actions?' (actions allowed)':' (read only)')+'\n'+tx.slice(0,3500); }catch(e){ return '### '+a.name+'\nUNAVAILABLE: '+e.message; } }));
    return '\n\nCONNECTED APPS (live data fetched just now):\n'+res.join('\n\n'); }
  async q1RunCall(c){ const a=this.q1Apps().find(x=>x.name===c.app);
    if(!a||!a.on||!a.actions){ return this.botSaveHist([...this.botHist(), {role:'bot', text:'Actions are switched off for '+(c.app||'that app')+'. Turn them on under Ledger › Q1 connected apps.', at:Date.now()}]); }
    if(!window.confirm('Q1 wants to run on '+a.name+':\n\n'+(c.method||'POST')+' '+(c.path||'/')+'\n'+(c.body?JSON.stringify(c.body,null,1).slice(0,400):'')+'\n\nRun it?')) return;
    this.setState({botThinking:true});
    let msg; try{ const tx=await this.q1Req(a, c.method||'POST', c.path, c.body); msg='Done on '+a.name+'. Response: '+tx.slice(0,300); }catch(e){ msg='That didn\'t go through on '+a.name+': '+e.message; }
    this.logAdd({ref:'Q1', customer:'—', amount:0, type:'Q1 APP ACTION', date:this.todayStamp(), by:(this.state.prof&&this.state.prof.name)||'—', comment:a.name+' · '+(c.method||'POST')+' '+(c.path||'/')+' · '+msg.slice(0,120)});
    this.botSaveHist([...this.botHist(), {role:'bot', text:msg, at:Date.now()}]); this.setState({botThinking:false}); }
  qkApp(){ return this.q1Apps().find(a=>/quik1/i.test(a.name)); }
  qkMap(r){ const g=(...k)=>{ for(const x of k){ const v=x.split('.').reduce((o,p)=>o==null?o:o[p], r); if(v!=null && v!=='') return v; } return ''; };
    const num=v=>parseFloat(String(v||'').replace(/[^\d.\-]/g,''))||0;
    const nm=g('name','fullName','full_name','applicant.name','client.name') || [g('firstName','first_name'),g('lastName','last_name','surname')].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
    const ext=String(g('id','applicationId','application_id','ref','reference'));
    if(!ext || !nm) return null;
    const docs=g('docs','documents'); 
    return {id:'qk_'+ext, extId:ext, name:nm, phone:String(g('phone','mobile','cell','contact','phoneNumber')||'—'), email:String(g('email')||'—'), idNumber:String(g('idNumber','id_number','saId','nationalId')||'—'),
      amount:num(g('amount','loanAmount','loan_amount','requested')), term:parseInt(g('term','months','instalments','installments'))||1, purpose:String(g('purpose','reason')||'Personal'),
      employer:String(g('employer','employment.employer','company')||'—'), jobTitle:String(g('jobTitle','job_title','occupation','employment.title')||'—'), income:num(g('income','netIncome','net_income','salary','netSalary')),
      bank:String(g('bank','bankName','bank_name')||'—'), docs:Array.isArray(docs)?docs.map(d=>typeof d==='string'?d:(d.name||d.type||'Document')):[],
      status:'new', source:'Quik1 app · via Q1', submitted:(()=>{ const s=g('submitted','createdAt','created_at','date'); const d=s?new Date(s):null; return d&&!isNaN(d)?d.toLocaleDateString('en-ZA',{day:'2-digit',month:'short',year:'numeric'}).toUpperCase():this.todayStamp(); })()}; }
  async qkSync(manual){
    const a=this.qkApp(); if(!a||!a.on||!a.url){ if(manual){ this.setState({modal:'q1apps', modalStack:[], q1Edit:a&&a.id}); this.toast('Link the Quik1 loan app first'); } return; }
    if(this._qkBusy) return; this._qkBusy=true;
    try{ const tx=await this.q1Req(a,'GET',a.path||'/applications'); let j; try{ j=JSON.parse(tx); }catch(e){ throw new Error('Quik1 did not return JSON'); }
      const rows=Array.isArray(j)?j:(j.applications||j.data||j.items||j.results||[]);
      const have=new Set((this.state.applications||[]).map(x=>x.id));
      const fresh=rows.map(r=>this.qkMap(r)).filter(x=>x && !have.has(x.id) && !/declin|approv|reject|cancel/i.test(String(rows.find(r=>String(r.id||r.applicationId||r.application_id||r.ref||r.reference)===x.extId)?.status||'')));
      fresh.forEach(x=>{ const c=(this.state.clients||[]).find(k=>(x.idNumber!=='—'&&k.idFull===x.idNumber)||(x.phone!=='—'&&String(k.phone).replace(/\D/g,'').slice(-9)===String(x.phone).replace(/\D/g,'').slice(-9))); if(c) x.existingClientId=c.id; });
      this._qkAt=Date.now(); this._qkErr=null;
      if(fresh.length){
        this.setState(p=>({applications:[...fresh, ...p.applications]}));
        fresh.forEach(x=>this.logAdd({ref:'APP-'+x.extId, customer:x.name, amount:x.amount, type:'APPLICATION RECEIVED', date:this.todayStamp(), by:'Q1', comment:'Quik1 app · '+(x.existingClientId?'returning client':'new applicant')}));
        const f=n=>this.fmt(n), L=fresh.slice(0,6).map(x=>'• '+x.name+' · '+f(x.amount)+(x.term>1?' over '+x.term+' months':'')+(x.existingClientId?' · returning client':''));
        this.botSaveHist([...this.botHist(), {role:'bot', text:fresh.length+' new loan application'+(fresh.length===1?'':'s')+' from the Quik1 app:\n'+L.join('\n')+(fresh.length>6?'\n…and '+(fresh.length-6)+' more':''), act:{go:'applications', label:'Review applications'}, at:Date.now(), brief:true, alert:true}]);
        this.toast('Q1 brought in '+fresh.length+' application'+(fresh.length===1?'':'s')+' from Quik1');
      } else if(manual) this.toast('No new Quik1 applications');
    }catch(e){ this._qkErr=e.message; if(manual) this.toast('Quik1: '+e.message); }
    finally{ this._qkBusy=false; this.forceUpdate(); } }
  qkPush(app, status){ const a=this.qkApp(); if(!app||!app.extId||!a||!a.on||!a.actions||!a.url) return;
    const base=String(a.path||'/applications').split('?')[0];
    this.q1Req(a,'PATCH',base.replace(/\/+$/,'')+'/'+encodeURIComponent(app.extId),{status}).then(()=>this.toast('Quik1 updated: '+app.name+' '+status)).catch(e=>this.toast('Quik1 not updated: '+e.message)); }
  async botAsk(text){
    const q=String(text||'').trim(); if(!q) return;
    const hist=[...this.botHist(), {role:'user', text:q, at:Date.now()}];
    this.botSaveHist(hist); this.setState({botDraft:'', botThinking:true});
    const ai = this.state.ai || {};
    const useKey = !!(ai.connected && ai.key);
    if(!useKey && !(window.claude && window.claude.complete)){
      this.botSaveHist([...hist, {role:'bot', text:'I can\'t reach Claude from here yet. Add your key under Settings › AI Document Reader, then ask me again.', at:Date.now()}]);
      return this.setState({botThinking:false});
    }
    const convo = hist.slice(-10).map(m=>(m.role==='user'?'User: ':'Q1: ')+m.text).join('\n');
    const prompt = 'You are Q1, the built-in assistant for LEDGER, a South African micro-lending console run by Ecoloans. Be brief, warm and practical: 1–4 short sentences or a tight list. Plain text only: no Markdown, no asterisks, no headings; use • for list items. Use rand as "R 1 234.56".\n'+
      'RULES: Answer only from the DATA below. Loan figures are the uploaded CSV values and are authoritative: quote them exactly, never recalculate, round, estimate or invent amounts. If something is not in the data, say so. You cannot change LEDGER records yourself; tell the user where to do it.\n'+
      'CONNECTED APPS: data from linked apps may appear below. Use it to answer questions about those apps and say which app a fact came from. If an app marked (actions allowed) should do something, end your reply with ONE line exactly like: APICALL: {"app":"<app name>","method":"POST","path":"/path","body":{},"label":"<button text>"}. The user must approve it before it runs.\n'+
      'If a screen would help, end your reply with ONE line exactly like: ACTION: {"go":"<target>","label":"<button text>"} where target is one of: dashboard, loans, clients, applications, employees, logbook, ledger, settings, notifications, bank, expenses, newloan, filter:duetoday, filter:overdue, filter:paid, filter:issued. Otherwise omit it.\n\nDATA:\n'+this.botContext()+(await this.q1Context())+'\n\nCONVERSATION:\n'+convo+'\nQ1:';
    let out='';
    const hasBuiltIn = !!(window.claude && window.claude.complete);
    try{
      out = useKey ? String(await this.callAI({provider:ai.provider||'claude', key:ai.key}, '', [], prompt)||'').trim()
                   : String(await window.claude.complete(prompt)||'').trim();
    }catch(e){
      const why = String(e.message||e);
      console.warn('Q1 AI error:', why);
      if(useKey && hasBuiltIn){
        try{ out = String(await window.claude.complete(prompt)||'').trim(); this._botFallback = why; }
        catch(e2){ out = 'Sorry, I couldn\'t get an answer just now. Your key returned: '+why.slice(0,160); }
      } else {
        out = 'Sorry, I couldn\'t get an answer. '+(useKey?'Your key returned: ':'')+why.slice(0,160)+(useKey?'\nCheck the key and account credit under Settings › AI.':'');
      }
    }
    let act=null; const mc=out.match(/APICALL:\s*(\{[^\n]*\})\s*$/);
    if(mc){ try{ const c=JSON.parse(mc[1]); act={go:'apicall', call:c, label:c.label||('Run on '+c.app)}; }catch(e){} out=out.slice(0,mc.index).trim(); }
    const m=act?null:out.match(/ACTION:\s*(\{[^\n]*\})\s*$/);
    if(m){ try{ act=JSON.parse(m[1]); }catch(e){} out=out.slice(0,m.index).trim(); }
    out = out.replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g,'$1').replace(/__(.+?)__/g,'$1').replace(/(^|\s)\*(\S[^*]*?)\*(?=\s|$)/g,'$1$2').replace(/`([^`]+)`/g,'$1').replace(/^#+\s*/gm,'').replace(/^\s*[-*]\s+/gm,'• ');
    this.botSaveHist([...this.botHist(), {role:'bot', text:out||'…', act, at:Date.now()}]);
    this.setState({botThinking:false});
  }
  botRun(act){
    if(!act||!act.go) return; const g=String(act.go);
    if(g==='apicall') return this.q1RunCall(act.call||{});
    if(g.indexOf('filter:')===0){ this.setState({filter:g.slice(7)}); return this.nav('loans'); }
    if(g==='bulkstatus' || (g==='notifications' && act.label==='Update statuses')) return this.setState({modal:'bulkstatus', modalStack:[], bsSel:{}, bsTo:'paid'});
    if(g==='notifications') return this.setState({modal:'notif', modalStack:[]});
    if(g==='employees'){ if(this.state.route!=='ledger'||!this.state.ledgerUnlocked) return this.nav('ledger'); return this.setState({admPane:'employees'}); }
    if(g==='bank'||g==='expenses'){ if(this.state.route!=='ledger'||!this.state.ledgerUnlocked) return this.nav('ledger'); return this.setState({modal:g, modalStack:[]}); }
    this.nav(g);
  }
  bankTx(){ if(!this._btx){ try{ this._btx=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ledjaa.bank.tx')||'[]'); }catch(e){ this._btx=[]; } } return this._btx; }
  bankTrack(){
    const loans=this.state.loans||[]; if(!this.state.dbReady && !loans.length) return;
    const snap={}; loans.forEach(l=>snap[l.id]=l.status);
    let prev=this._bankSnap;
    if(!prev){ try{ prev=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ledjaa.bank.snap')||'null'); }catch(e){} }
    if(!prev){ this._bankSnap=snap; try{ localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.bank.snap',JSON.stringify(snap)); }catch(e){} return; }
    const tx=this.bankTx(), add=[], now=Date.now(), by=(this.state.prof&&this.state.prof.name)||'—';
    const newIds = loans.filter(l=>!(l.id in prev));
    const bulk = newIds.length>3;
    loans.forEach(l=>{
      const was=prev[l.id], is=l.status, full=this.dueAmt(l);
      if(!(l.id in prev)){ if(!bulk && is!=='paid' && this.lentOf(l)!=null) add.push({type:'out', amount:this.lentNum(l), ref:l.ref, label:'Loan issued', at:now, by}); return; }
      if(was!=='paid' && is==='paid') add.push({type:'in', amount:full, ref:l.ref, label:'Collected · marked paid', at:now, by});
      if(was==='paid' && is!=='paid') add.push({type:'out', amount:full, ref:l.ref, label:'Reversed · unmarked paid', at:now, by});
    });
    this._bankSnap=snap;
    try{ localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.bank.snap',JSON.stringify(snap)); }catch(e){}
    if(add.length){ this._btx=[...add, ...tx].slice(0,500); try{ localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.bank.tx',JSON.stringify(this._btx)); }catch(e){} this.forceUpdate(); }
  }
  bankNow(){
    const b=this.bankCfg();
    if(b.balance==null){
      const nPaid=l=>l.paidInst!=null ? l.paidInst : (l.status==='paid'?1:0);
      const paid=(this.state.loans||[]).filter(l=>nPaid(l)>0);
      const inMoves=paid.map(l=>({type:'in', amount:Math.round(this.dueAmt(l)*nPaid(l)*100)/100, ref:l.ref, label:nPaid(l)>1?(nPaid(l)+' instalments paid · in bank'):'Paid · in bank', at:(this.state.statusAt||{})[l.id]||Date.now()}));
      const outMoves=this.bankTx().filter(t=>t.label==='Loan issued');
      const moves=[...inMoves, ...outMoves].sort((a,c)=>c.at-a.at);
      return {bal:Math.round((inMoves.reduce((a,t)=>a+t.amount,0)-outMoves.reduce((a,t)=>a+t.amount,0))*100)/100, moves, auto:true};
    }
    const moves=this.bankTx().filter(t=>t.at>b.at);
    return {bal:Math.round((b.balance+moves.reduce((a,t)=>a+(t.type==='in'?t.amount:-t.amount),0))*100)/100, moves};
  }
  bankSet(bal, note){
    const b=this.bankCfg(), entry={balance:bal, at:Date.now(), by:(this.state.prof&&this.state.prof.name)||'—', note:note||''};
    this._bank = {balance:bal, at:entry.at, by:entry.by, history:[entry, ...(b.history||[])].slice(0,30)};
    try{ localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.bank', JSON.stringify(this._bank)); }catch(e){}
    this.logAdd({ref:'BANK', customer:'—', amount:bal, type:'BANK BALANCE UPDATED', date:this.todayStamp(), by:entry.by, comment:note||'Balance confirmed'});
    this.sbQueue && this.sbQueue();
    this.toast('Bank balance updated');
    this.forceUpdate();
  }
  bulkItems(){
    const st=this.state, tn=this.today(), t0=new Date(tn.getFullYear(),tn.getMonth(),tn.getDate()).getTime(), out=[];
    (st.loans||[]).forEach(l=>{ if(l.status==='paid'||l.status==='written off') return;
      const ds=(l.sched&&l.sched.length?l.sched:[l.due]), i=Math.min(l.paidInst||0, ds.length-1), p=this.slParse(ds[i]); if(!p) return;
      const pt=new Date(p.getFullYear(),p.getMonth(),p.getDate()).getTime(); if(pt>t0) return;
      out.push({loan:l, instNo:(l.instDone||0)+i+1, total:(l.instDone||0)+ds.length, date:ds[i], pt}); });
    return out.sort((a,b)=>a.pt-b.pt);
  }
  notifItems(){
    const st=this.state, tn=this.today(), t0=new Date(tn.getFullYear(),tn.getMonth(),tn.getDate()).getTime();
    const out=[];
    st.loans.forEach(l=>{
      if(l.status==='paid'||l.status==='written off') return;
      const dates=(l.sched&&l.sched.length?l.sched:[l.due]);
      let todayIdx=-1, lastPast=null;
      const skip=l.paidInst||0, off=l.instDone||0;
      dates.forEach((d,i)=>{ if(i<skip) return; const p=this.slParse(d); if(!p) return; const pt=new Date(p.getFullYear(),p.getMonth(),p.getDate()).getTime();
        if(pt===t0) todayIdx=i; else if(pt<t0) lastPast={i,pt,d}; });
      const upd=(st.statusAt||{})[l.id]||0;
      if(todayIdx>=0) out.push({loan:l, kind:'today', instNo:off+todayIdx+1, total:off+dates.length, date:dates[todayIdx], flagged:false});
      else if(lastPast && (l.status==='issued'||l.status==='duetoday') && upd < lastPast.pt+864e5)
        out.push({loan:l, kind:'stale', instNo:off+lastPast.i+1, total:off+dates.length, date:lastPast.d, flagged:true, days:Math.round((t0-lastPast.pt)/864e5)});
    });
    return out.sort((a,b)=>(b.flagged-a.flagged));
  }
  async brUnzip(buf){
    const u=new Uint8Array(buf), dv=new DataView(buf), out={};
    let e=u.length-22; while(e>=0 && dv.getUint32(e,true)!==0x06054b50) e--; if(e<0) throw new Error('Not a valid .xlsx file');
    const cnt=dv.getUint16(e+10,true); let p=dv.getUint32(e+16,true);
    for(let i=0;i<cnt;i++){ const m=dv.getUint16(p+10,true), cs=dv.getUint32(p+20,true), nl=dv.getUint16(p+28,true), xl=dv.getUint16(p+30,true), cl=dv.getUint16(p+32,true), lo=dv.getUint32(p+42,true);
      const name=new TextDecoder().decode(u.slice(p+46,p+46+nl)); p+=46+nl+xl+cl;
      if(!/^xl\/(sharedStrings|worksheets\/sheet1|workbook)\.xml$/.test(name)) continue;
      const ds=lo+30+dv.getUint16(lo+26,true)+dv.getUint16(lo+28,true), raw=u.slice(ds,ds+cs);
      out[name]= m===0 ? new TextDecoder().decode(raw) : await new Response(new Blob([raw]).stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('deflate-raw'))).text(); }
    return out;
  }
  brXml(s){ return String(s||'').replace(/&lt;/g,'<').replace(/&gt;/g,'>').replace(/&quot;/g,'"').replace(/&apos;/g,"'").replace(/&amp;/g,'&'); }
  async brRead(file){
    let grid=[];
    if(/\.xlsx$/i.test(file.name)){
      const z=await this.brUnzip(await file.arrayBuffer()), ss=z['xl/sharedStrings.xml']||'', sh=z['xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml']||'';
      const strs=[...ss.matchAll(/<si>([\s\S]*?)<\/si>/g)].map(m=>[...m[1].matchAll(/<t[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/t>/g)].map(x=>this.brXml(x[1])).join(''));
      const col=c=>c.split('').reduce((a,ch)=>a*26+ch.charCodeAt(0)-64,0)-1;
      grid=[...sh.matchAll(/<row[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/row>/g)].map(r=>{ const a=[]; [...r[1].matchAll(/<c r="([A-Z]+)\d+"([^>]*?)(?:\/>|>([\s\S]*?)<\/c>)/g)].forEach(c=>{
        const v=(c[3]||'').match(/<v>([\s\S]*?)<\/v>/), is=(c[3]||'').match(/<t[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/t>/);
        a[col(c[1])]= v ? (/t="s"/.test(c[2])?strs[+v[1]]:this.brXml(v[1])) : (is?this.brXml(is[1]):''); }); return a; });
    } else {
      const txt=await file.text(), first=txt.split(/\r?\n/)[0]||'', d=(first.match(/\t/g)||[]).length>1?'\t':(first.match(/;/g)||[]).length>(first.match(/,/g)||[]).length?';':',';
      grid=txt.split(/\r?\n/).filter(x=>x.trim()).map(line=>{ const a=[]; let cur='',q=false; for(const ch of line){ if(ch==='"') q=!q; else if(ch===d&&!q){ a.push(cur); cur=''; } else cur+=ch; } a.push(cur); return a; });
    }
    const hi=grid.findIndex(r=>(r||[]).some(x=>/mandate\s*id/i.test(String(x||''))));
    if(hi<0) throw new Error('No "Mandate ID" column found');
    let H=grid[hi].map(x=>String(x||'').trim().toLowerCase()), sec='', out=[];
    const secOf=t=>/^success/i.test(t)?'Success':/^failed/i.test(t)?'Failed':/tracking/i.test(t)?'In Tracking':/^revers/i.test(t)?'Reversed':/^cancel/i.test(t)?'Cancelled':'';
    for(let ri=0; ri<grid.length; ri++){ const r=grid[ri]||[], cells=r.map(x=>String(x??'').trim()), filled=cells.filter(Boolean);
      if(filled.length===1 && secOf(filled[0])){ sec=secOf(filled[0]); continue; }
      if(cells.some(x=>/^mandate\s*id$/i.test(x))){ H=cells.map(x=>x.toLowerCase()); continue; }
      if(ri<=hi) continue;
      const o={}; H.forEach((k,i)=>o[k]=cells[i]||''); if(!o['mandate id'] || !/\d/.test(o['mandate id'])) continue;
      if(!o['status'] && sec) o['status']=sec; o._section=sec; out.push(o); }
    return out;
  }
  brIso(v){ v=String(v||'').trim(); if(/^\d{5}(\.\d+)?$/.test(v)){ const d=new Date(Math.round((+v-25569)*864e5)); return d.toISOString().slice(0,10); } const m=v.match(/(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/); return m?m[0]:''; }
  brLabel(iso){ const p=String(iso||'').split('-'); return p.length===3 ? (+p[2])+' '+['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'][+p[1]-1]+' '+p[0] : iso; }
  brAnalyse(raw){
    const st=this.state, clients=st.clients||[];
    return raw.map((o,i)=>{
      const mid=o['mandate id'], idno=o['debtor id'], res=o['status']||'', K=parseInt(o['instalment'],10)||1, V=parseInt(o['total instalments'],10)||1;
      const num=v=>parseFloat(String(v||'').replace(/[^\d.\-]/g,''))||0, deb=num(o['instalment amount']), orig=num(o['original amount']), pif=num(o['pif value']);
      const cycle=this.brIso(o['cycle date']||o['action date']), first=this.brIso(o['date of first instalment']);
      const outcome=(/^success/i.test(res)||o._section==='Success'||/paid|processed|honou?red/i.test(res))?'paid':(/at issuer|tracking|pending|submitted|loaded/i.test(res)||o._section==='In Tracking')?'pending':/fail|unpaid|reject|insuff|disput|revers|stop|return|cancel/i.test(res)?'unpaid':'pending';
      const l=(st.loans||[]).find(x=>String(x.mandate||'')===String(mid));
      const cli=l ? clients.find(c=>c.id===l.clientId) : clients.find(c=>String(c.idFull)===String(idno));
      const nameRaw=String(o['client reference']||'').replace(/^ecoloans\s*/i,'').trim();
      const r={key:'r'+i, mid, idno, res, outcome, K, V, deb, orig, pif, cycle, first, nameRaw, clientId:cli?cli.id:null, clientName:cli?cli.name:nameRaw, on:true, warn:''};
      if(!l){ r.kind='new'; r.on=outcome!=='pending'||true; r.action='Not on the book · add as new loan ('+V+' instalment'+(V>1?'s':'')+')'; return r; }
      r.loanId=l.id; r.ref=l.ref;
      const ds=(l.sched&&l.sched.length?l.sched:[l.due]); let idx=K-1-(l.instDone||0);
      if(idx<0||idx>=ds.length){ const ci=ds.findIndex(d=>{ const t=this.slParse(d); return t && t.toISOString && cycle && (t.getFullYear()+'-'+String(t.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(t.getDate()).padStart(2,'0'))===cycle; }); idx=ci; }
      r.idx=idx; r.dueLbl=idx>=0?ds[idx]:this.brLabel(cycle);
      const w=[]; if(orig && l.amount!=null && Math.abs(orig-l.amount)>.009) w.push('report return '+this.fmt(orig)+' ≠ book '+this.fmt(l.amount));
      if(deb && l.retAmt!=null && Math.abs(deb-l.retAmt)>.009) w.push('report debit '+this.fmt(deb)+' ≠ book '+this.fmt(l.retAmt));
      r.warn=w.length?w.join(' · ')+' — book amounts kept':'';
      if(idx<0){ r.kind='skip'; r.on=false; r.action='Instalment '+K+' not found on schedule'; return r; }
      const pd=l.paidInst||0;
      if(outcome==='paid'){ if(idx<pd||l.status==='paid'){ r.kind='skip'; r.on=false; r.action='Inst '+K+'/'+((l.instDone||0)+ds.length)+' already paid'; } else { r.kind='paid'; r.action='Mark inst '+K+'/'+((l.instDone||0)+ds.length)+' paid'+(idx>pd?' (and '+(idx-pd)+' earlier)':''); } }
      else if(outcome==='unpaid'){ r.kind='unpaid'; r.action='Mark overdue · debit '+res.toLowerCase(); }
      else { r.kind='skip'; r.on=false; r.action='Pending at bank · no change'; }
      return r; });
  }
  async brFile(e){
    const f=e&&e.target&&e.target.files&&e.target.files[0]; if(!f) return;
    try{ const raw=await this.brRead(f); if(!raw.length) return this.toast('No transactions found in '+f.name);
      this.setState({br:{file:f.name, rows:this.brAnalyse(raw)}}); }
    catch(err){ this.toast('Could not read '+f.name+': '+err.message); }
    try{ e.target.value=''; }catch(_){}
  }
  brApply(){
    const st=this.state, br=st.br; if(!br) return; const rows=br.rows.filter(r=>r.on && r.kind!=='skip'); if(!rows.length) return this.toast('Nothing selected to apply');
    const by=(st.prof&&st.prof.name)||'—', src=' · bank report '+br.file, now=Date.now();
    let loans=(st.loans||[]).slice(), clients=(st.clients||[]).slice(), cSeq=st.clientSeq, logs=[], sAt={...(st.statusAt||{})}, n={paid:0,unpaid:0,new:0};
    let fMax=loans.reduce((m,l)=>{ const x=/^ECL-F(\d+)$/.exec(l.ref||''); return x?Math.max(m,+x[1]):m; },0);
    rows.forEach(r=>{
      if(r.kind==='new'){
        let cid=r.clientId; if(!cid){ cid='c'+now+r.key; const cref=this.nextClientRef ? this.nextClientRef() : 'EC-0'+cSeq; clients.push({id:cid, ref:cref, name:r.nameRaw||'Unknown', idFull:r.idno||'—', added:this.todayLabel(), phone:'—', city:'—', employment:'—', employer:'—', netSalary:0}); cSeq++;
          logs.push({ref:cref, customer:r.nameRaw, amount:0, type:'CLIENT ADDED', date:this.todayStamp(), by, comment:'Created from'+src}); }
        const fd=r.first||r.cycle, p=fd.split('-').map(Number), sched=[]; for(let k=0;k<r.V;k++){ const d=new Date(p[0],p[1]-1+k,p[2]); sched.push(d.getDate()+' '+['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'][d.getMonth()]+' '+d.getFullYear()); }
        const paidN = r.outcome==='paid' ? Math.min(r.K, r.V) : Math.max(0, r.K-1);
        const ref='ECL-F'+String(++fMax).padStart(2,'0'), id='l'+now+r.key;
        loans.push(this.instNorm({id, ref, clientId:cid, amount:r.orig, retAmt:r.deb, instDone:0, instTotal:r.V, instRemaining:r.V-paidN, paidInst:paidN, ret:r.pif, status:r.outcome==='unpaid'?'overdue':(paidN>=r.V?'paid':'issued'), due:sched[Math.min(paidN,r.V-1)], purpose:'Personal', mandate:r.mid, sched, issued:this.brLabel(fd)}));
        sAt[id]=now; n.new++;
        logs.push({ref, customer:r.clientName, amount:r.orig, type:'LOAN ADDED', date:this.todayStamp(), by, comment:'Mandate '+r.mid+' · '+r.V+' × '+this.fmt(r.deb)+src});
        return; }
      const i=loans.findIndex(l=>l.id===r.loanId); if(i<0) return; const l=loans[i];
      if(r.kind==='paid'){ loans[i]=this.instNorm({...l, paidInst:Math.max(l.paidInst||0, r.idx+1), status:'issued'}); n.paid++;
        logs.push({ref:l.ref, customer:r.clientName, amount:l.amount, type:'PAYMENT RECEIVED', date:this.todayStamp(), by, comment:'Inst '+r.K+' · '+r.dueLbl+' · '+r.res+src}); }
      else if(r.kind==='unpaid'){ loans[i]={...l, status:'overdue'}; n.unpaid++;
        logs.push({ref:l.ref, customer:r.clientName, amount:l.amount, type:'STATUS → Overdue', date:this.todayStamp(), by, comment:'Inst '+r.K+' · '+r.dueLbl+' · '+r.res+src}); }
      sAt[l.id]=now; });
    this.setState({loans, clients, clientSeq:cSeq, statusAt:sAt, br:null, modal:null, modalStack:[]});
    logs.forEach(g=>this.logAdd(g));
    this.logAdd({ref:'BANK-'+rows.length, customer:'—', amount:0, type:'BANK REPORT IMPORTED', date:this.todayStamp(), by, comment:n.paid+' paid · '+n.unpaid+' unpaid · '+n.new+' new'+src});
    this.toast([n.paid?n.paid+' paid':'', n.unpaid?n.unpaid+' unpaid':'', n.new?n.new+' new loan'+(n.new>1?'s':''):''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||'Applied');
  }
  setLoanStatus(id, s, note){
    const l=this.state.loans.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!l||l.status===s) return;
    const c=this.state.clients.find(x=>x.id===l.clientId)||{};
    const sm=this.statusMeta(s);
    this.setState(p=>({loans:p.loans.map(x=>x.id===id?{...x,status:s}:x), statusAt:{...(p.statusAt||{}), [id]:Date.now()}}));
    this.logAdd({ref:l.ref, customer:c.name, amount:l.amount, type:'STATUS → '+sm.label, date:this.todayStamp(), by:this.state.prof.name, comment:note||'Updated from notifications'});
    if(!this._bsQuiet) this.toast(l.ref+' → '+sm.label);
  }
  nextCollectionDay(){
    const st = this.state, out = [];
    st.loans.forEach(l=>{
      if(l.status==='paid' || l.status==='written off') return;
      (l.sched && l.sched.length ? l.sched : [l.due]).forEach(dstr=>{ const t=this.slParse(dstr); if(t) out.push({dstr, t}); });
    });
    out.sort((a,b)=>a.t-b.t);
    const now = this.today();
    const future = out.filter(x=>x.t>=now);
    return (future[0] || out[0] || {}).dstr || null;
  }
  openDueBrief(dayKey, auto){
    const key = dayKey || this.nextCollectionDay();
    if(!key) return;
    this.setState({modal:'duebrief', dueBriefDay:key, dueBriefAuto:!!auto});
  }
  statusMeta(s){
    const map = {issued:['ISSUED','var(--blue)','var(--blueSoft)'],duetoday:['DUE TODAY','var(--amber)','var(--amberSoft)'],overdue:['OVERDUE','var(--red)','var(--redSoft)'],paid:['PAID','var(--green)','var(--greenSoft)']};
    const m = map[s] || [String(s).toUpperCase(),'var(--mut)','var(--hover)'];
    return {label:m[0], color:m[1], soft:m[2], style:{cssText:"display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font:600 10px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif;letter-spacing:.05em;padding:3px 9px;border-radius:99px;white-space:nowrap;background:"+m[2]+";color:"+m[1]}};
  }
  cssObj(t){ return {cssText:t}; }
  logAdd(entry){ this.setState(st=>({logbook:[entry,...st.logbook]})); }
  affordability(fmt){
    const st=this.state;
    const c = st.clients.find(x=>x.id===st.nf.client) || {};
    const net = c.netSalary || 0;
    const obl = ['expenses','ccDebt','existing','rent','groceries'].reduce((s,k)=>s+(parseFloat(String(st.nf[k]).replace(/\s/g,''))||0),0);
    const termMonths = {'1m':1,'2m':2,'3m':3,'6m':6}[st.nf.term] || 1;
    const ret = 20;
    const amt = parseFloat(String(st.nf.amount).replace(/\s/g,''))||0;
    const due = Math.round(amt*(1+ret/100));
    const repay = termMonths ? Math.round(due/termMonths) : 0;
    const disposable = net - obl - repay;
    const dtiRatio = net ? (obl+repay)/net : 0;
    const maxRepay = net*0.33;
    const maxLoan = net ? Math.max(0, Math.floor((maxRepay*termMonths/(1+ret/100))/500)*500) : 0;
    const pct = Math.round(dtiRatio*100)+'%';
    let verdict, reason, color;
    if(!net){ verdict='NO INCOME'; reason='Select a client with captured income to run the affordability check.'; color='var(--dim)'; }
    else if(!amt){ verdict='ENTER AMOUNT'; reason='Enter a loan amount to see the live verdict.'; color='var(--dim)'; }
    else if(disposable>0 && dtiRatio<=0.55 && repay<=maxRepay){ verdict='APPROVE'; reason='Within safe limits — '+fmt(disposable)+' disposable after this repayment.'; color='var(--green)'; }
    else if(disposable>0 && dtiRatio<=0.70){ verdict='REFER'; reason='Borderline at '+pct+' DTI — refer to a senior officer or trim the amount toward '+fmt(maxLoan)+'.'; color='var(--amber)'; }
    else { verdict='DECLINE'; reason = disposable<=0 ? 'Repayment exceeds disposable income by '+fmt(Math.abs(disposable))+'. Max affordable ≈ '+fmt(maxLoan)+'.' : 'DTI '+pct+' exceeds the 70% ceiling. Max affordable ≈ '+fmt(maxLoan)+'.'; color='var(--red)'; }
    return {net,obl,repay,disposable,dtiRatio,pct,maxLoan,verdict,reason,color,termMonths};
  }
  sizeSig(c){ c=c||this.sigCanvas; if(!c) return; const r=c.getBoundingClientRect(); if(!r.width) return;
    const d=window.devicePixelRatio||1, w=Math.round(r.width*d), h=Math.round(r.height*d);
    if(c.dataset.sized && Math.abs(c.width-w)<=3 && Math.abs(c.height-h)<=3) return; // fractional flex jitter must never wipe ink
    let snap=null;
    if(c.dataset.sized && c.width>0 && c.height>0){ snap=document.createElement('canvas'); snap.width=c.width; snap.height=c.height; snap.getContext('2d').drawImage(c,0,0); }
    c.width=w; c.height=h; c.dataset.sized='1';
    const x=c.getContext('2d');
    if(snap) x.drawImage(snap,0,0,w,h);
    x.setTransform(d,0,0,d,0,0); x.lineCap='round'; x.lineJoin='round'; x.strokeStyle='#e8eae6'; }
  activeSig(){ return this.state.sigFull ? this.fsCanvas : this.sigCanvas; }
  sigStart(e){ const c=this.activeSig(); if(!c) return;
    try{ e.target.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); }catch(err){}
    this._drawing=true;
    const rect=c.getBoundingClientRect();
    this._last={x:e.clientX-rect.left, y:e.clientY-rect.top};
    const pt=e.pointerType||'mouse'; if(pt!==this.state.sigPen) this.setState({sigPen:pt}); }
  sigDraw(e){ if(!this._drawing) return; const c=this.activeSig(); if(!c) return;
    const x=c.getContext('2d'), rect=c.getBoundingClientRect();
    const ne=e.nativeEvent||e;
    const evs=(ne.getCoalescedEvents && ne.getCoalescedEvents().length) ? ne.getCoalescedEvents() : [ne];
    evs.forEach(ev=>{ const nx=ev.clientX-rect.left, ny=ev.clientY-rect.top;
      const p=(ev.pressure!==undefined && ev.pressure>0) ? ev.pressure : 0.5;
      x.lineWidth=Math.max(1.2, Math.min(5.5, p*4.6));
      x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(this._last.x, this._last.y); x.lineTo(nx, ny); x.stroke();
      this._last={x:nx, y:ny}; });
    if(!this.state.sigInk) this.setState({sigInk:true}); }
  sigEnd(e){ this._drawing=false; if(e){ try{ e.target.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId); }catch(err){} } }
  sigWipe(){ const c=this.activeSig(); if(c){ const x=c.getContext('2d'); x.save(); x.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0); x.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height); x.restore(); } this.setState({sigInk:false}); }
  sigAccept(){
    const t=this.state.sigTarget;
    if(t){ const fs=this.fsCanvas;
      if(!(fs && this.state.sigInk)) { this.toast('Sign first'); return; }
      const url=fs.toDataURL(); const cli=this.state.clients.find(c=>c.id===t)||{};
      this.setState(p=>({clients:p.clients.map(c=>c.id===t?{...c, contract:{...(c.contract||{no:'MC-00'+(31+p.clients.indexOf(c)),signedOn:this.todayLabel()}), sig:url, signedOn:this.todayLabel()}}:c), sigFull:false, sigTarget:null, sigInk:false}));
      const x=fs.getContext('2d'); x.save(); x.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0); x.clearRect(0,0,fs.width,fs.height); x.restore();
      this.logAdd({ref:cli.ref||'—', customer:cli.name||'—', amount:0, type:'SIGNATURE UPDATED', date:this.todayStamp(), by:this.state.prof.name, comment:'Master agreement re-signed on tablet'});
      this.toast('Signature updated on '+((cli.contract&&cli.contract.no)||'master agreement'));
      return; }
    const fs=this.fsCanvas, inl=this.sigCanvas;
    if(fs && inl && this.state.sigInk){ this.sizeSig(inl); const x=inl.getContext('2d'); const d=window.devicePixelRatio||1;
      x.save(); x.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0); x.clearRect(0,0,inl.width,inl.height);
      const s=Math.min(inl.width/fs.width, inl.height/fs.height);
      x.drawImage(fs, (inl.width-fs.width*s)/2, (inl.height-fs.height*s)/2, fs.width*s, fs.height*s); x.restore(); }
    this.setState({sigFull:false}); }
  bulkExtract(text, clients){
    const lines = String(text||'').split(/\r?\n/).map(l=>l.trim()).filter(l=>l && !/^[-=_]+$/.test(l));
    if(!lines.length) return {rows:[], mapping:[], delim:','};
    const cands = [',',';','\t','|'];
    const delim = cands.map(d=>({d, n:lines.reduce((s,l)=>s+l.split(d).length-1,0)})).sort((a,b)=>b.n-a.n)[0].d;
    const split = l => l.split(delim).map(c=>c.replace(/^["']|["']$/g,'').trim());
    const norm = s => String(s||'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]/g,'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    const MONTHS=['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'];
    const KEY = {
      client:/client|customer|name|borrower|member|surname/i,
      amount:/amount|amt|principal|loan value|capital|advance|disbur|r *value|total loan/i,
      term:/term|month|duration|period|tenor|instal/i,
      due:/due|repay|maturit|deadline|pay ?back|settle|date.*(due|repay)/i,
      purpose:/purpose|reason|use|note|comment|descri/i,
      ref:/ref|id|account|number|no\.?$/i
    };
    const looksDate = s => /^\d{4}-\d{1,2}-\d{1,2}$/.test(s) || /^\d{1,2}[\/.-]\d{1,2}[\/.-]\d{2,4}$/.test(s) || /\d{1,2}\s*[a-z]{3,9}\s*\d{2,4}/i.test(s);
    const parseDate = s => {
      s = String(s||'').trim(); if(!s) return null;
      let m;
      if((m=s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})$/))) return {d:+m[3], mo:+m[2]-1, y:+m[1]};
      if((m=s.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\/.-](\d{1,2})[\/.-](\d{2,4})$/))) return {d:+m[1], mo:+m[2]-1, y:(+m[3]<100?2000+ +m[3]:+m[3])};
      if((m=s.match(/(\d{1,2})\s*([a-z]{3,9})\.?\s*(\d{2,4})?/i))){
        const mo = MONTHS.findIndex(x=>m[2].toLowerCase().startsWith(x.toLowerCase()));
        if(mo>=0) return {d:+m[1], mo, y:m[3]?(+m[3]<100?2000+ +m[3]:+m[3]):2026};
      }
      return null;
    };
    const fmtDate = o => o ? (String(o.d).padStart(2,'0')+' '+MONTHS[o.mo]+' '+o.y) : null;
    const money = s => { const v=String(s||'').replace(/[^\d.,]/g,'').replace(/,(?=\d{3}\b)/g,'').replace(/,/g,'.'); const n=parseFloat(v); return isFinite(n)?Math.round(n):0; };
    const dist = (a,b)=>{ if(a===b) return 0; const m=a.length,n=b.length; if(!m||!n) return Math.max(m,n);
      let prev=Array.from({length:n+1},(_,j)=>j);
      for(let i=1;i<=m;i++){ const cur=[i]; for(let j=1;j<=n;j++) cur[j]=Math.min(prev[j]+1, cur[j-1]+1, prev[j-1]+(a[i-1]===b[j-1]?0:1)); prev=cur; }
      return prev[n]; };
    const strip = s => String(s||'').replace(/\b(eco[\s-]?loans?|ledjaa|e[\s-]?volt)[\s\-–—:_,]*/gi,'').replace(/\becl\b[\s\-–—:_,]*/gi,'').replace(/\s{2,}/g,' ').trim();
    const matchClient = raw0 => {
      const raw = strip(raw0);
      const k = norm(raw); if(!k) return null;
      let c = clients.find(x=>norm(x.name)===k); if(c) return {c, how:'exact'};
      c = clients.find(x=>norm(x.ref)===k) || clients.find(x=>String(x.idFull).replace(/\s/g,'')===String(raw).replace(/\s/g,'')); if(c) return {c, how:'ref'};
      const toks = k.split(' ').filter(t=>t.length>2);
      c = clients.find(x=>{ const nt=norm(x.name).split(' '); return toks.length && toks.every(t=>nt.some(n2=>n2.startsWith(t)||t.startsWith(n2))); }); if(c) return {c, how:'fuzzy'};
      let best=null, bd=99;
      clients.forEach(x=>{ const d=dist(k, norm(x.name)); if(d<bd){ bd=d; best=x; } });
      if(best && bd<=Math.max(2, Math.floor(k.length*0.25))) return {c:best, how:'fuzzy'};
      return null;
    };

    // header detection
    const first = split(lines[0]);
    const hits = Object.keys(KEY).filter(k=>first.some(c=>KEY[k].test(c)));
    const hasHeader = hits.length>=2;
    const body = lines.slice(hasHeader?1:0).map(split).filter(r=>r.some(Boolean));
    const nCols = Math.max(...body.map(r=>r.length), first.length);
    const map = {};
    const mapping = [];
    const srcOf = i => hasHeader ? (first[i]||('col '+(i+1))) : ('col '+(i+1));
    if(hasHeader){
      first.forEach((h,i)=>{ const k=Object.keys(KEY).find(k2=>KEY[k2].test(h) && map[k2]===undefined);
        if(k){ map[k]=i; mapping.push({field:k, source:h||('col '+(i+1)), sure:true}); } });
    }
    // content scoring — every column scored against every field, best-match assigned
    const colVals = [];
    for(let i=0;i<nCols;i++) colVals[i] = body.map(r=>r[i]).filter(v=>v!==undefined && v!=='').slice(0,12);
    const rate = (i, fn) => colVals[i].length ? colVals[i].filter(fn).length/colVals[i].length : 0;
    const SCORE = {
      due: i=>rate(i, looksDate),
      client: i=>rate(i, v=>!!matchClient(v))*0.9 + rate(i, v=>/[a-z]{3}/i.test(v) && !looksDate(v) && !money(v))*0.1,
      amount: i=>rate(i, v=>money(v)>=500 && !looksDate(v)),
      term: i=>rate(i, v=>{ const n=parseInt(String(v).replace(/[^\d]/g,''),10); return n>=1 && n<=12 && !looksDate(v) && money(v)<500; })
    };
    const pairs = [];
    ['client','amount','due','term'].forEach(f=>{ if(map[f]!==undefined) return;
      for(let i=0;i<nCols;i++){ if(Object.values(map).includes(i)) continue;
        const s = SCORE[f](i); if(s>0.15) pairs.push({f, i, s}); } });
    pairs.sort((a,b)=>b.s-a.s || a.i-b.i);
    pairs.forEach(p=>{ if(map[p.f]!==undefined || Object.values(map).includes(p.i)) return;
      map[p.f]=p.i; mapping.push({field:p.f, source:srcOf(p.i), sure:p.s>=0.8}); });
    // required fields claim the best remaining column rather than staying unmapped
    ['client','amount'].forEach(f=>{ if(map[f]!==undefined) return;
      let best=-1, bs=-1;
      for(let i=0;i<nCols;i++){ if(Object.values(map).includes(i)) continue;
        const s=SCORE[f](i); if(s>bs){ bs=s; best=i; } }
      if(best>=0){ map[f]=best; mapping.push({field:f, source:srcOf(best), sure:false}); } });
    // purpose last — only takes a column nothing else claimed
    if(map.purpose===undefined){
      for(let i=0;i<nCols;i++){ if(Object.values(map).includes(i)) continue;
        if(rate(i, v=>/[a-z]{3}/i.test(v) && !looksDate(v))>0.4){ map.purpose=i; mapping.push({field:'purpose', source:srcOf(i), sure:hasHeader}); break; } }
    }
    const unmapped = ['client','amount'].filter(f=>map[f]===undefined);

    const used = Object.keys(map).map(k=>map[k]);
    const rows = body.map(cells=>{
      const extra = cells.map((v,i)=>({label:srcOf(i), value:v, i})).filter(x=>used.indexOf(x.i)<0 && x.value);
      const notes=[]; let conf=100;
      const g = f => map[f]!==undefined ? cells[map[f]] : '';
      const rawClient = strip(g('client'));
      const cm = matchClient(g('client'));
      if(cm && cm.how==='fuzzy'){ conf-=22; notes.push('Matched "'+rawClient+'" → '+cm.c.name); }
      else if(cm && cm.how==='ref'){ notes.push('Resolved by reference'); conf-=4; }
      const amount = money(g('amount'));
      let term = parseInt(String(g('term')||'').replace(/[^\d]/g,''),10)||0;
      if(/day/i.test(g('term')||'') && term>=25) { term=Math.max(1,Math.round(term/30)); notes.push('Converted days → '+term+' month'); conf-=8; }
      if(!term){ term=1; notes.push('Term missing — defaulted to 1 month'); conf-=14; }
      else if(![1,2,3,6].includes(term)){ const snapped=[1,2,3,6].reduce((a,b)=>Math.abs(b-term)<Math.abs(a-term)?b:a); notes.push('Term '+term+'m snapped to '+snapped+'m'); term=snapped; conf-=16; }
      let dueObj = parseDate(g('due'));
      let due = fmtDate(dueObj);
      if(!due){ const base=new Date(2026,6,20); base.setMonth(base.getMonth()+term); due=fmtDate({d:base.getDate(), mo:base.getMonth(), y:base.getFullYear()}); notes.push('Due date inferred from term'); conf-=18; }
      const purpose = String(g('purpose')||'').trim();
      if(!purpose) conf-=4;
      const errs=[];
      if(!cm) errs.push('No client match for "'+(rawClient||'blank')+'"');
      if(!amount) errs.push('No amount found');
      else if(amount<500||amount>200000) errs.push('Amount outside R500–R200 000');
      return { client: cm?cm.c.name:(rawClient||'—'), clientId: cm?cm.c.id:null, amount, term, due,
        purpose: purpose||'—', conf: errs.length ? Math.max(10, Math.min(conf,40)-errs.length*10) : Math.max(35, conf),
        bad: errs.length>0, error: errs.join(' · '), notes, extra, raw: cells.join(delim===''?',':delim) };
    });
    const FLBL={client:'client name',amount:'loan amount'};
    const alert = unmapped.length ? ("Couldn't identify the "+unmapped.map(f=>FLBL[f]).join(' or ')+" column — check the file has that data, or add a header row.") : '';
    return { rows, mapping, delim, hasHeader, unmapped, alert };
  }
  bulkParse(text, clients){
    const lines = String(text||'').split(/\r?\n/).map(l=>l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if(!lines.length) return [];
    const start = /client/i.test(lines[0]) && /amount/i.test(lines[0]) ? 1 : 0;
    const norm = s => String(s||'').toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    return lines.slice(start).map(line=>{
      const cells = line.split(/[,;\t]/).map(c=>c.trim());
      const [rawClient, rawAmt, rawTerm, rawDue, ...rest] = cells;
      const purpose = rest.join(', ').trim();
      const key = norm(rawClient);
      const match = clients.find(c=>norm(c.name)===key) || clients.find(c=>norm(c.ref)===key) || clients.find(c=>String(c.idFull)===String(rawClient).trim()) || clients.find(c=>norm(c.name).startsWith(key)&&key.length>3);
      const amount = Math.round(parseFloat(String(rawAmt||'').replace(/[^\d.]/g,''))||0);
      const term = parseInt(String(rawTerm||'').replace(/[^\d]/g,''),10)||0;
      const due = String(rawDue||'').trim();
      const errs = [];
      if(!match) errs.push('No client matching "'+(rawClient||'—')+'"');
      if(!amount) errs.push('Amount missing or invalid');
      else if(amount<500||amount>200000) errs.push('Amount outside R500–R200 000');
      if(![1,2,3,6].includes(term)) errs.push('Term must be 1, 2, 3 or 6 months');
      if(!due) errs.push('Due date missing');
      return { client: match?match.name:(rawClient||'—'), clientId: match?match.id:null, amount, term, due: due||'—', purpose,
        bad: errs.length>0, error: errs.join(' · ') };
    });
  }
  navIcon(r, active){
    const P = { notifications:['M6 8a6 6 0 0 1 12 0c0 7 3 9 3 9H3s3-2 3-9','M10.3 21a1.94 1.94 0 0 0 3.4 0'], dashboard:['M4 4h7v6H4z','M13 4h7v9h-7z','M13 16h7v4h-7z','M4 13h7v7H4z'], loans:['M3 6h18v12H3z','M3 10.5h18','M6.5 15h4'], clients:['M17 20v-1a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H8a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v1','M10.5 11a3.4 3.4 0 1 0 0-6.8 3.4 3.4 0 0 0 0 6.8'], employees:['M4 5h16v14H4z','M12 11.6a2.3 2.3 0 1 0 0-4.6 2.3 2.3 0 0 0 0 4.6','M8.6 16.4a3.4 3.4 0 0 1 6.8 0'], logbook:['M8 6h12','M8 12h12','M8 18h12','M4 6h.01','M4 12h.01','M4 18h.01'], ledger:['M4 20h16','M6.5 20v-6','M11 20V8','M15.5 20v-9'], petty:['M3 8.5h15a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v6.5a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V7a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h11','M16 13.5h.5'], applications:['M4 13h4l1.5 3h5L16 13h4','M4 13V6a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h12a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v7','M4 13v5a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h12a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-5'] };
    const paths = (P[r]||[]).map((d,i)=>React.createElement('path',{key:i,d}));
    return React.createElement('svg',{width:18,height:18,viewBox:'0 0 24 24',fill:'none',stroke:'currentColor',strokeWidth:1.7,strokeLinecap:'round',strokeLinejoin:'round',style:{flex:'none',color: active?'var(--accText)':'var(--mut)'}}, paths);
  }
  fileToBlock(file){
    const isPdf = file.type==='application/pdf' || /\.pdf$/i.test(file.name||'');
    if(isPdf){
      return new Promise((resolve)=>{
        const r = new FileReader();
        r.onload = ()=>resolve({type:'document',source:{type:'base64',media_type:'application/pdf',data:String(r.result).split(',')[1]}});
        r.onerror = ()=>resolve(null);
        r.readAsDataURL(file);
      });
    }
    // Normalise every image (incl. HEIC/odd/empty MIME types) through a canvas to a capped JPEG
    return new Promise((resolve)=>{
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = ()=>{
        try{
          const cap = 1600, scale = Math.min(1, cap/Math.max(img.width,img.height));
          const w = Math.max(1,Math.round(img.width*scale)), h = Math.max(1,Math.round(img.height*scale));
          const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width=w; cv.height=h;
          const cx = cv.getContext('2d'); cx.fillStyle='#fff'; cx.fillRect(0,0,w,h); cx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
          const data = cv.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.9).split(',')[1];
          URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
          resolve(data ? {type:'image',source:{type:'base64',media_type:'image/jpeg',data}} : null);
        }catch(e){ URL.revokeObjectURL(url); resolve(null); }
      };
      img.onerror = ()=>{ URL.revokeObjectURL(url); resolve(null); };
      img.src = url;
    });
  }
  mockExtract(){
    return {firstName:'Nomvula', lastName:'Sithole', gender:'Female', marital:'Single', email:'nomvula.sithole@mail.co.za', contact:'074 118 6620', dob:'1993-08-14', idNumber:'9308140455083', address:'42 Marula Street', city:'Midrand', province:'Gauteng', postal:'1685',
      employmentType:'Permanent', payFreq:'Monthly', grossSalary:'24500', netSalary:'19180', employer:'Dimension Data', jobTitle:'Systems Analyst', employerContact:'011 575 0000', jobDuration:'3',
      bank:'Standard Bank', branchCode:'051001', accountNumber:'041228876', accountType:'Cheque', accountDuration:'5'};
  }
  async pdfToText(file){
    try{
      const mod = await import('https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/pdf-parse@2.4.5/dist/pdf-parse/web/pdf-parse.es.js');
      const P = mod.PDFParse; P.setWorker('https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/pdf-parse@2.4.5/dist/pdf-parse/web/pdf.worker.min.mjs');
      const buf = new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer());
      const parser = new P({ data: buf });
      const r = await parser.getText();
      return (r && r.text) ? r.text : '';
    }catch(e){ console.warn('PDF text extraction failed:', e); return ''; }
  }
  localExtract(text){
    const t = String(text).replace(/\u00a0/g,' ');
    const num = s => String(s).replace(/[^\d]/g,'').replace(/(\d+)\d{2}$/, '$1');
    const out = {};
    const id = t.match(/\b(\d{13})\b/);
    if(id){ out.idNumber = id[1];
      const yy=id[1].slice(0,2), mm=id[1].slice(2,4), dd=id[1].slice(4,6);
      out.dob = (parseInt(yy,10)>30?'19':'20')+yy+'-'+mm+'-'+dd;
      out.gender = parseInt(id[1].slice(6,10),10)<5000?'Female':'Male';
    }
    const nm = t.match(/(?:Mr|Mrs|Ms|Miss|Dr)\.?\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'-]+)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'-]+)/);
    if(nm){ out.firstName = nm[1]; out.lastName = nm[2]; }
    const em = t.match(/[\w.+-]+@[\w-]+\.[\w.-]+/); if(em) out.email = em[0];
    const ph = t.match(/(?:\+?27|0)\s?\d{2}\s?\d{3}\s?\d{4}/); if(ph) out.contact = ph[0].replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    const net = t.match(/Net Pay\s*[:\s]*R?\s*([\d ,]+\.\d{2})/i); if(net) out.netSalary = num(net[1]);
    const gross = t.match(/Total Earnings\s*[:\s]*R?\s*([\d ,]+\.\d{2})/i); if(gross) out.grossSalary = num(gross[1]);
    const acc = t.match(/Acc(?:ount)?\s*(?:Number|No)?\s*[:\s]*(\d{6,})/i); if(acc) out.accountNumber = acc[1];
    const job = t.match(/Job Title\s*[:\s]*([A-Za-z][A-Za-z \-\/]{2,40})/i); if(job) out.jobTitle = job[1].trim();
    if(/Monthly/i.test(t)) out.payFreq = 'Monthly';
    if(/Permanent/i.test(t)) out.employmentType = 'Permanent';
    return out;
  }
  async callAI(cfg, docText, imageBlocks, prompt){
    const userText = (docText ? ('DOCUMENT TEXT (extracted on-device):\n'+docText+'\n\n') : '') + prompt;
    if(cfg.host){
      const content = [...imageBlocks, {type:'text', text:userText}];
      return await window.claude.complete({ model:'claude-haiku-4-5', max_tokens:1024, messages:[{role:'user', content}] });
    }
    if(cfg.provider==='openai'){
      const content = [{type:'text', text:userText}];
      for(const b of imageBlocks){ if(b.type==='image') content.push({type:'image_url', image_url:{url:'data:'+b.source.media_type+';base64,'+b.source.data}}); }
      const res = await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions', { method:'POST',
        headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+cfg.key},
        body: JSON.stringify({ model:'gpt-4o-mini', max_tokens:1024, messages:[{role:'user', content}] }) });
      if(!res.ok){ let msg=''; try{ const j=await res.json(); msg=(j.error&&j.error.message)||''; }catch(e){} throw new Error('OpenAI '+res.status+(msg?' — '+msg:'')); }
      const j = await res.json(); return j.choices && j.choices[0] && j.choices[0].message.content;
    }
    const content = [...imageBlocks, {type:'text', text:userText}];
    const res = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', { method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','x-api-key':cfg.key,'anthropic-version':'2023-06-01','anthropic-dangerous-direct-browser-access':'true'},
      body: JSON.stringify({ model:'claude-haiku-4-5', max_tokens:1024, messages:[{role:'user', content}] }) });
    if(!res.ok){ let msg=''; try{ const j=await res.json(); msg=(j.error&&j.error.message)||''; }catch(e){} throw new Error('Anthropic '+res.status+(msg?' — '+msg:'')); }
    const j = await res.json(); return j.content && j.content.map(c=>c.text||'').join('');
  }
  async analyzeDocs(files){
    const keys = ['firstName','lastName','gender','marital','email','contact','dob','idNumber','address','city','province','postal','dependents','employmentType','payFreq','grossSalary','netSalary','employer','jobTitle','employerContact','jobDuration','bank','branchCode','accountNumber','accountType','accountDuration'];
    const prompt = 'These are a loan applicant\'s documents (South African ID, payslip and/or bank statement). Extract every field you can read and return ONLY a JSON object with these keys: '+keys.join(', ')+'. Use gender values Female/Male/Other; marital Single/Married/Divorced/Widowed; employmentType Permanent/Contract/Self-employed/Informal; payFreq Monthly/Fortnightly/Weekly; accountType Cheque/Savings/Transmission; province the full SA province name; salary/amount fields as digits only (no R or spaces); dob as YYYY-MM-DD. Omit keys you cannot determine. Return no prose, only the JSON.';
    const textChunks = [], imageBlocks = [];
    for(const f of files){
      const isPdf = f.type==='application/pdf' || /\.pdf$/i.test(f.name||'');
      if(isPdf){
        const txt = await this.pdfToText(f);
        if(txt && txt.trim()) textChunks.push('=== '+(f.name||'document')+' ===\n'+txt.trim());
        else { const b = await this.fileToBlock(f); if(b) imageBlocks.push(b); }
      } else { const b = await this.fileToBlock(f); if(b) imageBlocks.push(b); }
    }
    const docText = textChunks.join('\n\n');
    if(!docText && !imageBlocks.length) return {__error:'no-file'};
    const ai = this.state.ai || {};
    const hasKey = !!(ai.connected && ai.key && ai.key.trim());
    const hasHost = !!(window.claude && window.claude.complete);
    if(hasKey || hasHost){
      try{
        const raw = await this.callAI({ provider: hasKey?ai.provider:'claude', key: hasKey?ai.key.trim():null, host: !hasKey && hasHost }, docText, imageBlocks, prompt);
        const m = String(raw||'').match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
        if(m){ const parsed = JSON.parse(m[0]); const clean={}; for(const k of keys) if(parsed[k]!=null && String(parsed[k]).trim()!=='') clean[k]=String(parsed[k]).trim(); if(Object.keys(clean).length) return clean; }
      }catch(e){ console.warn('AI read failed, trying on-device fallback:', e); }
    }
    if(docText){ const local = this.localExtract(docText); if(Object.keys(local).length) return {...local, __local:true}; }
    return {__error: (imageBlocks.length && !docText) ? 'no-ai' : 'empty'};
  }
  apiRand(n){ const c='abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789', a=new Uint8Array(n); try{ window.crypto.getRandomValues(a); }catch(e){ for(let i=0;i<n;i++) a[i]=Math.floor(Math.random()*256); } return Array.from(a,b=>c[b%c.length]).join(''); }
  apiData(){
    if(this.state.api) return this.state.api;
    if(!this._apiD){
      try{ this._apiD = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ledgerApiV1')||'null'); }catch(e){}
      if(!this._apiD){ const org=(this.props.orgName ?? (this._org&&this._org.name) ?? 'Ecoloans');
        this._apiD = { env:'live', ws:'ws_'+String(org).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'').slice(0,20), pk:{live:'pk_live_'+this.apiRand(24), test:'pk_test_'+this.apiRand(24)}, whsec:'whsec_'+this.apiRand(32), keys:[], hooks:[], domains:[], reqs:[] };
        try{ localStorage.setItem('ledgerApiV1', JSON.stringify(this._apiD)); }catch(e){} }
    }
    return this._apiD;
  }
  apiSet(fn){ const next=fn(this.apiData()); this._apiD=next; try{ localStorage.setItem('ledgerApiV1', JSON.stringify(next)); }catch(e){} this.setState({api:next}); }
  apiClock(){ const d=new Date(), MN=['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec']; return d.getDate()+' '+MN[d.getMonth()]+' · '+String(d.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0'); }
  apiLog(entries){ this.apiSet(a=>({...a, reqs:[...entries.map(e=>({...e, t:this.apiClock(), env:a.env})), ...a.reqs].slice(0,40)})); }
  apiEmit(event, app){
    const A=this.apiData(), hooks=A.hooks.filter(h=>h.events.includes(event));
    if(!hooks.length) return;
    this.apiSet(a=>({...a, hooks:a.hooks.map(h=>h.events.includes(event)?{...h, last:event+' · '+this.apiClock()}:h)}));
    this.apiLog(hooks.map(h=>({m:'POST', p:h.url, code:200, src:'Webhook · '+event+(app&&app.name?' · '+app.name:'')})));
  }
  apiCopy(t){ try{ navigator.clipboard.writeText(t).then(()=>this.toast('Copied'), ()=>this.toast('Copy failed — select and copy manually')); }catch(e){ this.toast('Copy failed — select and copy manually'); } }

        this.toast('Test application from '+s[0]+' is in Applications'); },
      apiEndpoints:EP.map(([m,p,d])=>({m, p, d, mStyle:this.cssObj("font:700 10px "+mono+";letter-spacing:.04em;"+(m==='POST'?'color:var(--accText)':'color:var(--blue)'))})),
      apiCurl:curl, apiCurlCopy:()=>this.apiCopy(curl.replace(/lk_(live|test)_••••\w{4}/,'lk_'+env+'_YOUR_SECRET_KEY')),
      apiReqs:A.reqs.map(r=>({time:r.t, m:r.m, p:r.p, code:String(r.code), src:r.src+(r.env==='test'?' · test':''),
        codeStyle:this.cssObj("font:600 11px "+mono+";color:"+(r.code<300?'var(--green)':r.code<500?'var(--amber,#e2a336)':'var(--red)'))})),
      apiReqsEmpty:A.reqs.length===0
    };
  }
  nav(route){ this.setState(p=>({route, menuId:null, modal:null, modalStack:[], admPane:null, ledgerView:null, ...(p.route==='ledger' && route!=='ledger' ? {ledgerUnlocked:false} : {})})); }
  pushModal(next){ this.setState(p=>({ modalStack:[...(p.modalStack||[]), {modal:p.modal, modalId:p.modalId}], smsOpen:false, smsText:'', cliEdit:null, ...next })); }
  openDate(target, dob){ const now=new Date(); this.setState({datePick:{target, month:dob?0:now.getMonth(), year:dob?1995:now.getFullYear(), dob:!!dob}}); }
  writeDate(d){ const dp=this.state.datePick; if(!dp) return;
    const MN=['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'];
    const val=String(d).padStart(2,'0')+' '+MN[dp.month]+' '+dp.year;
    const t=dp.target;
    if(t==='ql.due') this.setState(p=>({ql:{...p.ql, due:val}, datePick:null}));
    else if(t==='nf.due') this.setState(p=>({nf:{...p.nf, due:val}, datePick:null}));
    else if(t==='nf.salaryDate') this.setState(p=>({nf:{...p.nf, salaryDate:val}, datePick:null}));
    else if(t==='ac.dob') this.setState(p=>({ac:{...p.ac, dob:val}, datePick:null}));
    else if(t==='led.due') this.setState(p=>({led:{...p.led, due:val}, datePick:null}));
    else if(t==='led.issued') this.setState(p=>({led:{...p.led, issued:val}, datePick:null}));
    else this.setState({datePick:null});
  }
  contractHtml(c){
    const orgL = this.props.orgLegalName ?? (this._org && this._org.legal) ?? ((this.props.orgName ?? (this._org&&this._org.name) ?? 'Ecoloans')+' Finance (Pty) Ltd');
    const mk=(this.props.maskPii ?? true) && !this.state.revealed;
    const idShown = mk ? '••••••• '+String(c.idFull||'').slice(-4) : (c.idFull||'—');
    const fill=(s)=>String(s).split('{org}').join(orgL).split('{client}').join(c.name||'the client').split('{id}').join(idShown).split('{rate}').join('35%');
    const t=this.state.ctpl;
    const clauses=t.clauses.map((cl,i)=>'<p style="margin:0 0 10px;display:flex;gap:10px"><b style="color:#111">'+(i+1)+'.</b><span>'+fill(cl)+'</span></p>').join('');
    const sig=c.contract&&c.contract.sig;
    const sigBlock = sig ? '<img src="'+sig+'" style="height:60px"/>' : '<div style="border-bottom:1px solid #999;width:220px;height:40px"></div>';
    return '<div style="font-family:Georgia,\'Times New Roman\',serif;color:#111;line-height:1.7">'
      +'<div style="text-align:center;border-bottom:2px solid #111;padding-bottom:16px;margin-bottom:24px">'
      +'<div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.5px">'+orgL+'</div>'
      +'<div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:2px;color:#555;margin-top:6px">MASTER CREDIT AGREEMENT · '+((c.contract&&c.contract.no)||'—')+'</div></div>'
      +'<p style="margin:0 0 16px">'+fill(t.intro)+'</p>'+clauses
      +'<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin:22px 0;font-size:13px"><tr>'
      +'<td style="padding:8px 0"><b>Client:</b> '+(c.name||'—')+'</td><td style="padding:8px 0"><b>ID:</b> '+idShown+'</td></tr>'
      +'<tr><td style="padding:8px 0"><b>Debit account:</b> '+(c.bank||'—')+' · '+(c.account||'—')+'</td>'
      +'<td style="padding:8px 0"><b>Signed on:</b> '+(sig?((c.contract&&c.contract.signedOn)||'—'):'—')+'</td></tr></table>'
      +'<div style="margin-top:40px"><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:1px;color:#555;margin-bottom:6px">CLIENT SIGNATURE</div>'+sigBlock+'</div></div>';
  }
  slCode(ref){ const s=String(ref||''); let h=0; for(let i=0;i<s.length;i++){ h=(h*31+s.charCodeAt(i))>>>0; } return 'VC-'+s.replace(/\D/g,'').slice(-4)+'-'+h.toString(36).toUpperCase().slice(0,5); }
  qrSrc(text){ try{ if(!window.qrcode) return null; const q=window.qrcode(0,'M'); q.addData(text); q.make(); return q.createDataURL(5,0); }catch(e){ return null; } }
  slWords(n){
    const ones=['','One','Two','Three','Four','Five','Six','Seven','Eight','Nine','Ten','Eleven','Twelve','Thirteen','Fourteen','Fifteen','Sixteen','Seventeen','Eighteen','Nineteen'];
    const tens=['','','Twenty','Thirty','Forty','Fifty','Sixty','Seventy','Eighty','Ninety'];
    const u=(x)=>x<20?ones[x]:x<100?(tens[Math.floor(x/10)]+(x%10?'-'+ones[x%10]:'')):(ones[Math.floor(x/100)]+' Hundred'+(x%100?' and '+u(x%100):''));
    const big=(x)=>{ if(x===0) return 'Zero';
      const parts=[]; const mil=Math.floor(x/1000000), th=Math.floor((x%1000000)/1000), rest=x%1000;
      if(mil) parts.push(u(mil)+' Million'); if(th) parts.push(u(th)+' Thousand'); if(rest) parts.push((parts.length?'and ':'')+u(rest));
      return parts.join(' '); };
    const rand=Math.floor(n), cents=Math.round((n-rand)*100);
    return big(rand)+' Rand'+(cents?' and '+u(cents)+' Cents':' Only');
  }
  slDate(d){ const M=['January','February','March','April','May','June','July','August','September','October','November','December'];
    return d.getDate()+' '+M[d.getMonth()]+' '+d.getFullYear(); }
  slParse(s){ const M=['jan','feb','mar','apr','may','jun','jul','aug','sep','oct','nov','dec'];
    const m=String(s||'').trim().match(/(\d{1,2})\s+([A-Za-z]{3})[a-z]*\s+(\d{4})/);
    if(!m) return null; const mi=M.indexOf(m[2].toLowerCase()); if(mi<0) return null;
    return new Date(+m[3], mi, +m[1]); }
  settleData(l){
    const c = this.state.clients.find(x=>x.id===l.clientId) || {};
    const orgName = 'LEDGER';
    const orgL = 'Ecoloans';
    const issue = new Date(2026,6,20);
    const bd=(d,n)=>{ const x=new Date(d); let k=0; while(k<n){ x.setDate(x.getDate()+1); if(x.getDay()!==0&&x.getDay()!==6) k++; } return x; };
    const expires = bd(issue,5);
    const sub = this.dueAmt(l);
    const hasBank = c.bank && c.bank!=='—';
    const tpf = hasBank ? 241.25 : 0;
    const total = sub + tpf;
    const small = l.amount <= 15000;
    const paid = l.status==='paid';
    const code = this.slCode(l.ref);
    const no = (paid?'PU-':'SQ-')+String(l.ref||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
    const verifyUrl = 'verify.'+String(orgName).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'')+'.co.za/'+code;
    const collect = this.slParse(l.due);
    return { c, orgName, orgL, issue, expires, sub, tpf, total, small, paid, code, no, verifyUrl, collect,
      title: paid ? 'PAID-UP LETTER' : 'SETTLEMENT QUOTATION' };
  }
  settlementHtml(l){

    const mk=(this.props.maskPii ?? true) && !this.state.revealed;
    const idRaw = String(c.idFull||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
    const idFmt = idRaw.length===13 ? idRaw.slice(0,6)+' '+idRaw.slice(6,10)+' '+idRaw.slice(10) : (c.idFull||'—');

    const S = "font-family:'Helvetica Neue',Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif;color:#14181c";

    const summary = (k,v,accent)=>'<div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><div style="'+lab+'">'+k+'</div><div style="font-size:'+(accent?'15px':'11.5px')+';font-weight:700;margin-top:5px;'+(accent?'':'letter-spacing:.2px')+'">'+v+'</div></div>';

      +'<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;border-bottom:2px solid #14181c;padding-bottom:12px;margin-bottom:22px">'
