  printSettlement(l){
    const { d, p1, p2 } = this.settlementHtml(l);
    const w=window.open('','_blank','width=880,height=1100'); if(!w){ this.toast('Allow pop-ups to download the PDF'); return; }
    w.document.write('<html><head><title>'+d.no+' — '+(d.c.name||'')+'</title>'
      +'<style>@page{size:A4;margin:16mm}body{margin:0}.pg{max-width:178mm;margin:0 auto}.br{page-break-before:always}</style></head>'
      +'<body onload="window.focus();window.print();"><div class="pg">'+p1+'</div><div class="pg br">'+p2+'</div></body></html>');
    w.document.close();
    this.logAdd({ref:l.ref, customer:d.c.name||'—', amount:Math.round(d.total), type:d.paid?'PAID-UP LETTER':'SETTLEMENT QUOTATION', date:this.todayStamp(), by:this.state.prof.name, comment:d.no+' generated · verify '+d.code});
  }
  printPdf(c){
    const w=window.open('','_blank','width=820,height=1000'); if(!w){ this.toast('Allow pop-ups to download the PDF'); return; }
    w.document.write('<html><head><title>'+((c.contract&&c.contract.no)||'contract')+' — '+(c.name||'')+'</title>'
      +'<style>@page{size:A4;margin:22mm}body{margin:0}</style></head><body onload="window.focus();window.print();">'
      +'<div style="max-width:170mm;margin:0 auto">'+this.contractHtml(c)+'</div></body></html>');
    w.document.close();
    this.logAdd({ref:c.ref||'—', customer:c.name||'—', amount:0, type:'CONTRACT EXPORTED', date:this.todayStamp(), by:this.state.prof.name, comment:'Contract PDF generated'});
  }
  startCall(name, phone, ref){
    if(this.state.call){ this.toast('A call is already in progress'); return; }
    this.setState({callConfirm:{name, phone, ref}});
  }
  doCall(name, phone, ref){
    if(!this.state.twilio.connected){ this.toast('Connect Twilio in Settings first'); return; }
    if(this.state.call){ this.toast('A call is already in progress'); return; }
    clearInterval(this._callT);
    this.setState({call:{name, phone:phone||'—', ref:ref||'—', sec:0, status:'CONNECTING'}});
    this._callT = setInterval(()=>{ const c=this.state.call; if(!c) return clearInterval(this._callT);
      this.setState({call:{...c, sec:c.sec+1, status:c.sec>=1?'IN CALL':'CONNECTING'}}); }, 1000);
  }
  endCall(){
    const c=this.state.call; if(!c) return;
    clearInterval(this._callT);
    const dur = Math.floor(c.sec/60)+':'+String(c.sec%60).padStart(2,'0');
    this.logAdd({ref:c.ref, customer:c.name, amount:0, type:'CALL (TWILIO)', date:this.todayStamp(), by:this.state.prof.name, comment:'Outbound call · '+dur});
    this.setState(p=>({call:null, twilio:{...p.twilio, mins:p.twilio.mins+Math.max(1,Math.ceil(c.sec/60))}}));
    this.toast('Call ended · '+dur);
  }
  smsVia(name, ref, comment){
    if(!this.state.twilio.connected){ this.toast('Connect Twilio in Settings first'); return false; }
    this.logAdd({ref:ref||'—', customer:name, amount:0, type:'SMS (TWILIO)', date:this.todayStamp(), by:this.state.prof.name, comment:comment||'SMS sent via Twilio'});
    this.setState(p=>({twilio:{...p.twilio, sms:p.twilio.sms+1}}));
    this.toast('SMS sent via Twilio · delivery confirmed');
    return true;
  }
  isCsvLoan(l){ return !!l && l.retAmt!=null && !/^ECL-0\d/.test(l.ref||''); }
  dueAmt(l){ if(!l) return 0; if(this.isCsvLoan(l)) return l.amount||0; return Math.round((l.amount||0)*(1+((l.ret==null?35:l.ret))/100)*100)/100; }
  debitAmt(l){ if(!l) return 0; return this.isCsvLoan(l) ? l.retAmt : this.dueAmt(l); }
  pifAmt(l){ return this.isCsvLoan(l) ? Math.round((l.retAmt-(l.amount||0))*100)/100 : 0; }
  lentMap(){ if(!this._lentMap){ try{ this._lentMap=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ledjaa.lent')||'{}')||{}; }catch(e){ this._lentMap={}; } } return this._lentMap; }
  lentOf(l){ if(!l) return null; if(!this.isCsvLoan(l)) return l.amount||0; const m=this.lentMap(); const v = l.lent!=null ? l.lent : m[l.ref]; if(v!=null) return v; return l.amount ? Math.round(l.amount/1.35*100)/100 : null; }
  lentDerived(l){ return !!l && this.isCsvLoan(l) && l.lent==null && this.lentMap()[l.ref]==null && !!l.amount; }
  lentNum(l){ return this.lentOf(l)||0; }
  lentFmt(l){ const v=this.lentOf(l); return v==null ? '—' : this.fmt(v); }
  setLent(id){ const l=(this.state.loans||[]).find(x=>x.id===id); if(!l) return;
    const v=window.prompt('Amount actually lent to the client for '+l.ref+' (R):', l.lent==null?'':String(l.lent)); if(v===null) return;
    const n=parseFloat(String(v).replace(/[^\d.]/g,'')); if(isNaN(n)||n<=0) return this.toast('Enter an amount in rand');
    const m={...this.lentMap(), [l.ref]:n}; this._lentMap=m; try{ localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.lent', JSON.stringify(m)); }catch(e){}
    this.setState(p=>({loans:p.loans.map(x=>x.id===id?{...x, lent:n}:x)}));
    const c=(this.state.clients||[]).find(x=>x.id===l.clientId)||{};
    this.logAdd({ref:l.ref, customer:c.name||'—', amount:n, type:'AMOUNT LENT SET', date:this.todayStamp(), by:this.state.prof.name, comment:'Lent '+this.fmt(n)+' · return '+this.fmt(l.amount)+' per instalment'});
    this.toast(l.ref+' lent set to '+this.fmt(n)); }
  renderVals(){
    const st = this.roleScope(this.state), fmt = this.fmt.bind(this);
    const orgName = this.props.orgName ?? (this._org && this._org.name) ?? 'Ecoloans';
    const mono = "font:500 11.5px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif;letter-spacing:.08em;";
    const navDefs = [['dashboard','DASHBOARD','2px'],['loans','LOANS','2px'],['applications','APPLICATIONS','50%'],['clients','CLIENTS','50%'],['logbook','LOGBOOK','2px'],['ledger','LEDGER','2px'],['petty','PETTY CASH','50%'],['notifications','NOTIFICATIONS','50%']];
    const appNewCount = st.applications.filter(a=>a.status==='new').length;
    const notif = this.notifItems();
    const notifFlagged = notif.filter(n=>n.flagged).length;
    const navItems = navDefs.map(([r,label,rad])=>{
      const active = st.route===r || (r==='loans'&&['newloan'].includes(st.route)) || (r==='clients'&&st.route==='addclient');
      const nice = label.charAt(0)+label.slice(1).toLowerCase();
      const col = !!st.navCollapsed;
      return {label: col ? '' : nice, title: nice, go:()=>(r==='notifications'?this.setState({modal:'notif', modalStack:[], menuId:null}):this.nav(r)),
        style:this.cssObj("display:flex;align-items:center;"+(col?'justify-content:center;gap:0;padding:14px 0;':'gap:12px;padding:14px 16px;')+"border-radius:14px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;letter-spacing:-.01em;transition:background .2s ease, color .2s ease, transform .2s cubic-bezier(.22,.8,.28,1);font:"+(active?'700':'500')+" 13.5px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif;"+(active?'background:var(--ink);color:var(--bg)':'color:var(--mut)')),
        badge: (r==='notifications'&&notif.length>0) ? React.createElement('span',{style:{font:"700 10px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",background:'var(--red)',color:'#fff',boxShadow:'0 0 0 2px rgba(255,59,59,.18)',borderRadius:'99px',padding:'1px 7px',marginLeft:col?'-6px':'auto',lineHeight:1.5,alignSelf:col?'flex-start':'auto'}}, notif.length) : (r==='applications'&&appNewCount>0) ? React.createElement('span',{style:{font:"700 10px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",background:'var(--acc)',color:'var(--accInk)',borderRadius:'99px',padding:'1px 7px',marginLeft:'auto',lineHeight:1.5}}, appNewCount) : '',
        icon: this.navIcon(r, active)};
    });
    const clientById = id => st.clients.find(c=>c.id===id) || {};
    const tkMode = st.tickerMode || 'markets';
    const tkLive = st.loans.filter(l=>l.status!=='paid' && l.status!=='written off');
    const tkOverdue = st.loans.filter(l=>l.status==='overdue');
    let tkData;
    if(tkMode==='clients'){
      const byClient = st.clients.map(c=>{
        const cl = tkLive.filter(l=>l.clientId===c.id);
        return {name:c.name, out:cl.reduce((s,l)=>s+this.lentNum(l),0), n:cl.length, od:cl.filter(l=>l.status==='overdue').length};
      }).filter(x=>x.n>0).sort((a,b)=>b.out-a.out).slice(0,12);
      tkData = byClient.length ? byClient.map(x=>[String(x.name).toUpperCase(), fmt(x.out), x.od?(x.od+' OVERDUE'):(x.n+(x.n===1?' LOAN':' LOANS'))])
                               : [['NO ACTIVE CLIENTS','—','']];
    } else if(tkMode==='performance'){
      const outT = tkLive.reduce((s,l)=>s+this.lentNum(l),0);
      const expT = tkLive.reduce((s,l)=>s+this.dueAmt(l)*(l.instRemaining||1),0);
      const odT = tkOverdue.reduce((s,l)=>s+this.dueAmt(l),0);
      const paid = st.loans.filter(l=>l.status==='paid');
      const wo = st.loans.filter(l=>l.status==='written off');
      const settled = paid.length + wo.length;
      const avgRet = st.loans.length ? (st.loans.reduce((s,l)=>s+(l.ret==null?35:l.ret),0)/st.loans.length).toFixed(1)+'%' : '—';
      const avgTicket = tkLive.length ? fmt(outT/tkLive.length) : '—';
      tkData = [
        ['LOANED OUT', fmt(outT), ''],
        ['EXPECTED RETURN', fmt(expT), '+'+fmt(expT-outT)],
        ['OVERDUE', fmt(odT), tkOverdue.length?('-'+tkOverdue.length+' LOANS'):'0.0%'],
        ['ACTIVE LOANS', String(tkLive.length), ''],
        ['ACTIVE CLIENTS', String(st.clients.length), ''],
        ['AVG RETURN', avgRet, ''],
        ['AVG TICKET', avgTicket, ''],
        ['SETTLED', String(paid.length), settled?('+'+Math.round(paid.length/settled*100)+'% RECOVERY'):''],
        ['WRITTEN OFF', String(wo.length), wo.length?'-'+fmt(wo.reduce((s,l)=>s+this.lentNum(l),0)):'0.0%']
      ];
    } else {
      tkData=[['USD/ZAR','18.42','-0.3%'],['EUR/ZAR','20.05','+0.1%'],['GBP/ZAR','23.71','-0.2%'],['JSE ALSI','79 420','+0.6%'],['BRENT','$84.10','+0.4%'],['GOLD','$2 016','+0.2%'],['REPO','8.25%','0.0%'],['PRIME','11.75%','0.0%']];
    }
    const ticker = tkData.map(a=>({sym:a[0],val:a[1],delta:a[2]||'',dStyle:this.cssObj('color:'+(String(a[2]||'').charAt(0)==='+'?'var(--green)':String(a[2]||'').charAt(0)==='-'?'var(--red)':'var(--dim)'))}));
    const tkLabel = tkMode==='clients'?'CLIENTS':tkMode==='performance'?'BOOK':'MARKETS';
    const tkNext = ()=>{ const order=['markets','clients','performance']; this.setState({tickerMode: order[(order.indexOf(tkMode)+1)%3]}); };
    const MONTHS=['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'];
    const parseDue = str => { const m=String(str).match(/(\d{1,2})\s+([A-Za-z]{3})\s+(\d{4})/); if(!m) return null; const mi=MONTHS.findIndex(x=>x.toLowerCase()===m[2].slice(0,3).toLowerCase()); return {d:+m[1],mo:mi,y:+m[3]}; };
    const cy=st.calYear, cm=st.calMonth;
    const dim=new Date(cy,cm+1,0).getDate(), firstDow=new Date(cy,cm,1).getDay();
    const dueByDay={};
    st.loans.forEach(l=>{
      if(l.status==='paid' || l.status==='written off') return;
      const dates = (l.sched && l.sched.length ? l.sched : [l.due]);
      dates.forEach((dstr,i)=>{ if(i<(l.paidInst||0)) return; const p=parseDue(dstr);
        if(p && p.mo===cm && p.y===cy) (dueByDay[p.d]=dueByDay[p.d]||[]).push({...l, due:dstr, instNo:(l.instDone||0)+i+1}); });
    });
    const calCells=[];
    for(let i=0;i<firstDow;i++) calCells.push({day:'', has:false, style:this.cssObj('height:30px')});
    for(let d=1;d<=dim;d++){ const onDay=dueByDay[d]||[], has=onDay.length>0, selc=st.calSelected===d;
      const late=onDay.some(l=>l.status==='overdue'), today=onDay.some(l=>l.status==='duetoday');
      const tnow=this.today(), isTodayCell = tnow.getDate()===d && tnow.getMonth()===cm && tnow.getFullYear()===cy;
      const dotCol=selc?'var(--accInk)':isTodayCell?'#fff':late?'var(--red)':today?'var(--amber)':'var(--acc)';
      const dayKey = d+' '+MONTHS[cm]+' '+cy;
      const wkend=[0,6].includes(new Date(cy,cm,d).getDay());
      calCells.push({day:String(d), has, count:String(onDay.length),
        badgeStyle:this.cssObj("flex:none;min-width:11px;height:11px;padding:0 3px;box-sizing:border-box;border-radius:99px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font:800 7px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif;line-height:1;"+(late?'background:var(--red);color:#fff':today?'background:var(--amber);color:#111':'background:var(--line2);color:var(--ink)')),
        pick:()=>{ this.setState({calSelected:d}); if(onDay.length) this.openDueBrief(dayKey, false); },
        dots: onDay.slice(0,1).map(()=>({style:this.cssObj('width:4px;height:4px;border-radius:50%;background:'+dotCol)})),
        dueTip: onDay.length ? (onDay.length+(onDay.length===1?' payment · ':' payments · ')+fmt(onDay.reduce((t,l)=>t+this.dueAmt(l),0))) : '',
        style:this.cssObj('position:relative;width:100%;min-width:0;height:100%;min-height:0;font-size:clamp(8.5px,1.6vh,10.5px);line-height:1;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:3px;border-radius:9px;cursor:pointer;transition:background .18s ease, transform .18s cubic-bezier(.22,.8,.28,1), color .18s ease, filter .18s ease;font:'+(isTodayCell?'800':'500')+' 11px \'Plus Jakarta Sans\',sans-serif;'+(selc?'background:var(--acc);color:var(--accInk)':isTodayCell?'background:var(--blue);color:#fff;box-shadow:0 0 0 2px var(--blueSoft), 0 6px 14px -6px rgba(0,0,254,.6)':has?'background:var(--calCellHas);color:var(--calCellInk);border:1px solid var(--calCellHasLine);box-shadow:var(--calCellShadow)':wkend?'background:var(--calCellWk);color:var(--calCellDim);border:1px solid var(--calCellLine)':'background:var(--calCell);color:var(--calCellMut);border:1px solid var(--calCellLine);box-shadow:var(--calCellShadow)')+(selc&&isTodayCell?';box-shadow:0 0 0 2px var(--blue)':'')),
        dotRowStyle:this.cssObj('position:absolute;top:2px;right:2px;display:flex;gap:1px;pointer-events:none'),
        tipStyle:this.cssObj('position:absolute;top:50%;left:50%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);white-space:nowrap;background:var(--ink);color:var(--bg);border-radius:8px;padding:5px 8px;font:600 9.5px \'Plus Jakarta Sans\',sans-serif;opacity:0;pointer-events:none;transition:opacity .14s ease;z-index:8;box-shadow:var(--shadow)')}); }
    const selLoans = (st.calSelected && dueByDay[st.calSelected]) ? dueByDay[st.calSelected] : [];
    const calAgreements = selLoans.slice(0,3).map(l=>{ const c=clientById(l.clientId), sm=this.statusMeta(l.status); return {customer:c.name, ref:l.ref, master:(c.contract?c.contract.no:'—'), dueAmountFmt: fmt(this.dueAmt(l)), statusLabel:sm.label, statusStyle:sm.style, open:()=>this.setState({modal:'history',modalId:l.id})}; });
    const attnBase = st.loans.filter(l=>(l.status==='overdue'||l.status==='duetoday') && !st.attended[l.id]);
    const attnRows = attnBase.map(l=>{ const c=clientById(l.clientId); const note=l.status==='overdue'?'Overdue since '+l.due:'Due today'; return {customer:c.name, ref:l.ref, note, amountFmt:fmt(l.amount), dot:(l.status==='overdue'?'var(--red)':'var(--amber)'), open:()=>this.setState({attentionOpen:false, modal:'history', modalId:l.id}), resolve:()=>{ this.logAdd({ref:l.ref, customer:c.name, amount:l.amount, type:'ATTENDED', date:this.todayStamp(), by:st.prof.name, comment:'Opened from needs-attention'}); this.setState(p=>({attended:{...p.attended,[l.id]:true}, attentionOpen:false, route:'clientview', clientViewId:c.id, revealed:false, cliEdit:null})); }}; });
    const statOpen = st.loans.filter(l=>l.status!=='paid' && l.status!=='written off');
    const instOf = l => this.dueAmt(l);
    const upcoming = [];
    statOpen.forEach(l=>{ (l.sched && l.sched.length ? l.sched : [l.due]).forEach(dstr=>{
      const p=parseDue(dstr); if(p) upcoming.push({k:p.y*12+p.mo, d:p.d, label:dstr, amt:instOf(l), risk:(l.status==='overdue'), cid:l.clientId}); }); });
    upcoming.sort((a,b)=>(a.k-b.k)||(a.d-b.d));
    const nextKey = upcoming.length ? upcoming[0].label : null;
    const nextDueAmt = upcoming.filter(x=>x.label===nextKey).reduce((t,x)=>t+x.amt,0);
    const thisKey = upcoming.length ? upcoming[0].k : null;
    const thisMonth = upcoming.filter(x=>x.k===thisKey);
    const avgRet = statOpen.length ? (statOpen.reduce((t,l)=>t+(l.ret==null?35:l.ret),0)/statOpen.length) : 0;
    const monthTotals = {};
    upcoming.forEach(x=>{ const m = monthTotals[x.k] = monthTotals[x.k] || {amt:0, n:0, risk:0}; m.amt += x.amt; m.n++; if(x.risk) m.risk += x.amt; });
    const mKeys = Object.keys(monthTotals).map(Number).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
    const mMax = mKeys.reduce((t,k)=>Math.max(t, monthTotals[k].amt), 0) || 1;
    const attnChart = mKeys.map(k=>{ const m = monthTotals[k];
      const pct = Math.max(8, Math.round((m.amt/mMax)*100));
      const soon = k === mKeys[0];
      const riskPct = m.amt ? Math.round((m.risk/m.amt)*100) : 0;
      return {
        label: MONTHS[k%12].toUpperCase(),
        barStyle: this.cssObj('position:relative;width:100%;height:'+pct+'%;border-radius:8px 8px 3px 3px;overflow:hidden;transform-origin:bottom;animation:growBar .6s cubic-bezier(.22,.8,.28,1) both;background:'+(soon?'var(--acc)':'var(--accSoft)')+';transition:background .2s ease, transform .2s cubic-bezier(.22,.8,.28,1), filter .2s ease'),
        barHover: soon ? 'transform:scaleY(1.04);filter:brightness(1.06)' : 'background:var(--acc)',
        riskStyle: this.cssObj('position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;height:'+riskPct+'%;background:var(--red)'),
        amountFmt: fmt(m.amt),
        countLabel: m.n + (m.n===1?' INSTALMENT':' INSTALMENTS') + (m.risk? ' · '+fmt(m.risk)+' AT RISK' : ''),
        tipStyle: this.cssObj('position:absolute;bottom:26px;'+(k===mKeys[0]?'left:0;':(k===mKeys[mKeys.length-1]?'right:0;':'left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);'))+'white-space:nowrap;background:var(--ink);color:var(--bg);border-radius:10px;padding:6px 9px;opacity:0;pointer-events:none;transition:opacity .14s ease;z-index:6;box-shadow:var(--shadow)')
      }; });
    const attnChartHas = mKeys.length > 0;
    // --- secondary graphs on the EXPECTED RETURN card ---
    const gLive = st.loans.filter(l=>l.status!=='paid' && l.status!=='written off');
    const gPrin = gLive.reduce((s,l)=>s+this.lentNum(l),0);
    const gExp  = gLive.reduce((s,l)=>s+this.dueAmt(l)*(l.instRemaining||1),0);
    const gInt  = Math.max(0, gExp-gPrin);
    const gTot  = gPrin+gInt || 1;
    const splitBar = [
      {label:'PRINCIPAL', amt:this.fmt(gPrin), pct:Math.round(gPrin/gTot*100),
       barStyle:this.cssObj('height:100%;width:'+(gPrin/gTot*100)+'%;background:var(--acc);transition:width .6s cubic-bezier(.22,.8,.28,1)'),
       dotStyle:this.cssObj('width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:3px;background:var(--acc)')},
      {label:'INTEREST', amt:this.fmt(gInt), pct:Math.round(gInt/gTot*100),
       barStyle:this.cssObj('height:100%;width:'+(gInt/gTot*100)+'%;background:var(--blue);transition:width .6s cubic-bezier(.22,.8,.28,1)'),
       dotStyle:this.cssObj('width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:3px;background:var(--blue)')}
    ];
    const gByClient = st.clients.map(c=>{
      const cl = gLive.filter(l=>l.clientId===c.id);
      return {name:c.name, exp:cl.reduce((s,l)=>s+this.dueAmt(l)*(l.instRemaining||1),0), od:cl.some(l=>l.status==='overdue')};
    }).filter(x=>x.exp>0).sort((a,b)=>b.exp-a.exp).slice(0,5);
    const gMax = gByClient.length ? gByClient[0].exp : 1;
    const expByClient = gByClient.map((x,i)=>({
      name:x.name, amt:this.fmt(x.exp),
      barStyle:this.cssObj('height:8px;border-radius:99px;width:'+Math.max(4,x.exp/gMax*100)+'%;background:'+(x.od?'var(--red)':'var(--acc)')+';animation:growW .55s cubic-bezier(.22,.8,.28,1) '+(0.04*i)+'s both')
    }));
    const expByClientHas = expByClient.length>0;
    const arrangeMode = !!st.arrangeMode;
    const gridRef = el => { this._gridEl = el; if(el) setTimeout(()=>this.applyCardOrder(),0); };
    const toggleArrange = ()=>this.setState({arrangeMode:!arrangeMode});
    const arrangeLabel = arrangeMode ? 'DONE' : 'ARRANGE';
    const arrangeBtnStyle = this.cssObj('display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;padding:7px 13px;border-radius:99px;cursor:pointer;user-select:none;font:600 10px \'Plus Jakarta Sans\',sans-serif;letter-spacing:.09em;transition:background .22s ease,color .22s ease;'+(arrangeMode?'background:var(--acc);color:var(--accInk);border:1px solid transparent':'background:var(--panel3);color:var(--mut);border:1px solid var(--line2)'));
    const dragOn = arrangeMode;
    const dragStart = e => {
      if(!arrangeMode){ e.preventDefault(); return; }
      this._dragCard = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.card);
      e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='move';
      try{ e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', String(this._dragCard)); }catch(err){}
      e.currentTarget.style.opacity='.45';
    };
    const dragOver = e => {
      if(!arrangeMode || this._dragCard==null) return;
      e.preventDefault(); e.dataTransfer.dropEffect='move';
      const over = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.card);
      if(over===this._dragCard) return;
      const ord = this.cardOrder().slice();
      const a = ord.indexOf(this._dragCard), b = ord.indexOf(over);
      if(a<0||b<0) return;
      ord.splice(b,0,ord.splice(a,1)[0]);
      this._cardOrder = ord;
      this.applyCardOrder();
    };
    const dragDrop = e => { if(arrangeMode) e.preventDefault(); };
    const dragEnd = e => {
      e.currentTarget.style.opacity='';
      this._dragCard = null;
      try{ localStorage.setItem('ledjaa.cardOrder.v3', JSON.stringify(this.cardOrder())); }catch(err){}
      this.forceUpdate();
    };
    const nw = st.news || {items:[]};
    const nCat = st.newsCat || 'all';
    const nItems = (nw.items||[]).filter(n=>nCat==='all'||n.cat===nCat);
    const ago = t => { const m=Math.max(0,Math.round((Date.now()-t)/60000)); return m<1?'NOW':m<60?m+'M AGO':m<1440?Math.round(m/60)+'H AGO':Math.round(m/1440)+'D AGO'; };
    const nMeta = n => (n.src+' · '+ago(n.at)).toUpperCase();
    const dayAgo = Date.now()-24*36e5;
    const fresh = [...nItems].filter(n=>n.at>=dayAgo).sort((a,b)=>b.at-a.at);
    const pool = fresh.length ? fresh : [...nItems].sort((a,b)=>b.at-a.at);
    const dayKey = Math.floor((Date.now()+2*36e5)/864e5);
    const withImg = pool.filter(n=>n.img);
    const nLead = withImg.length ? withImg[dayKey % Math.min(withImg.length,3)] : null;
    const bgOf = u => u ? 'background-image:url("'+String(this.hiResImg(u)).replace(/"/g,'%22')+'"), url("'+String(u).replace(/"/g,'%22')+'");background-size:cover;background-position:center;' : '';

              const term=parseInt(r.term,10)||1, cname=clientById(r.clientId).name;
              const base={id:'l'+Date.now()+'-'+i, clientId:r.clientId, ret:35, status:'issued', issued:this.todayLabel(), due:r.due, term};

                patch[keep.id]={amount:mergedPrincipal, ret:0, due:r.due, term, purpose:'Consolidated debt'};
                act.slice(1).forEach(l=>{ close[l.id]={status:'written off', reason:'Consolidated into '+keep.ref+' — balance carried over, not collected'};

              }

        const files = this._docFiles || [];

        this.setState({docProcessing:true});
        this.toast('Assessing '+files.length+' document'+(files.length>1?'s':'')+'…');

          if(extracted && extracted.__error){
            this.setState({docProcessing:false});
            this.toast(extracted.__error==='no-ai' ? 'Photos/scans need an AI reader — add a Claude or OpenAI key in Settings, or upload a PDF.' : 'Couldn\u2019t read those documents. Try clearer scans, a PDF, or enter the fields manually.');
            return;
          }

          const clean={}; for(const k in extracted) if(k.slice(0,2)!=='__') clean[k]=extracted[k];
          const n = Object.keys(clean).length;
          this.setState(p=>{ const merged={...p.ac}; for(const k in clean){ if(String(clean[k]).trim()!=='') merged[k]=clean[k]; } return {ac:merged, docProcessing:false, docExtracted:true, docFieldCount:n}; });
          this.toast(n+' field'+(n===1?'':'s')+(local?' read on-device':' read from your documents')+' — please review');
        });

      revealLabel: st.revealed ? 'PII UNMASKED · CLICK TO HIDE' : 'REVEAL FULL PII (ADMIN)',
      revealBtnStyle: this.cssObj('font:500 10.5px \'Plus Jakarta Sans\',sans-serif;letter-spacing:.06em;border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid '+(st.revealed?'var(--acc);color:var(--accText)':'var(--line2);color:var(--mut)')),
      toggleReveal: ()=>{ if(!st.revealed){ const c=st.clients.find(x=>x.id===st.modalId); if(c) this.logAdd({ref:c.ref, customer:c.name, amount:0, type:'PII UNMASKED', date:this.todayStamp(), by:'Craig Moatshe', comment:'Full ID / account / salary viewed in Client Info'}); }
        this.setState(p=>({revealed:!p.revealed})); },
      delPin: st.delPin||'',
      delPinChange:(e)=>this.setState({delPin:e.target.value.replace(/\D/g,'').slice(0,4), delPinErr:false}),
      delPinErrMsg: st.delPinErr?'Incorrect PIN — nothing was deleted.':'',
      delPinStyle: this.cssObj("width:150px;box-sizing:border-box;text-align:center;letter-spacing:.5em;background:var(--panel);border:1px solid "+(st.delPinErr?'var(--red)':'var(--line2)')+";border-radius:12px;padding:12px 10px;font:700 18px 'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif;color:var(--ink)"),
      delBtnStyle: this.cssObj('flex:1;padding:12px;border-radius:12px;text-align:center;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;'+((st.delPin||'').length===4?'background:var(--red);color:#ffffff;cursor:pointer':'background:var(--panel2);color:var(--dim);cursor:not-allowed')),
      confirmDelete:()=>{ if(!mLoanRaw) return;
        if((st.delPin||'')!=='1244'){ return this.setState({delPinErr:true, delPin:''}); }
        this.logAdd({ref:mLoanRaw.ref, customer:clientById(mLoanRaw.clientId).name, amount:mLoanRaw.amount, type:'DELETE AUTHORISED', date:this.todayStamp(), by:st.prof.name, comment:'Manager PIN accepted for '+mLoanRaw.ref});
        this.setState(p=>({loans:p.loans.filter(l=>l.id!==mLoanRaw.id), modal:null, delPin:'', delPinErr:false}));
        this.logAdd({ref:mLoanRaw.ref, customer:clientById(mLoanRaw.clientId).name, amount:mLoanRaw.amount, type:'LOAN DELETED', date:this.todayStamp(), by:'Craig Moatshe', comment:'Deleted via console'});
        this.toast(mLoanRaw.ref+' deleted — logged'); }
    };
  }
}
